# Source File: src/webview/horizon_panel.html

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>Context Memory</title>
<link rel="icon" href="data:,">
<script src="/preload.js"></script>
<style>
  :root {
    /* Host IDE Bridge Overrides (Prevents #333 fallback bleed) */
    --background: #090d16;
    --foreground: #f8fafc;
    --muted: #111827;
    --muted-foreground: #94a3b8;
    --border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.10);
    --ring: #38bdf8;

    /* Surface Palette (Developer Slate / Zinc) */
    --hz-bg: #090d16;
    --hz-card: #0f1623;
    --hz-card-alt: #161f30;
    --hz-fg: #f8fafc;
    --hz-fg-secondary: #cbd5e1;
    --hz-muted: #94a3b8;         /* WCAG AA 7.1:1 against --hz-bg */
    --hz-dim: #94a3b8;           /* Strict WCAG AA >= 6.3:1 on all dark surfaces */
    --hz-border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.10);
    --hz-border-subtle: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);

    /* Semantic Telemetry Indicators */
    --hz-useful: #10b981;        /* Green: Active code + rules */
    --hz-cached-history: #0d9488;/* Muted Slate/Teal: Cached History — 95% Discounted */
    --hz-clutter: #f59e0b;       /* Amber: Reclaimable noise / Cold Bloat */
    --hz-danger: #ef4444;        /* Red: Over limit / errors */
    --hz-free: #1e293b;          /* Dark slate: Free headroom */
    --hz-anchor: #38bdf8;        /* Cyan: Rules & interactive links */
  }

  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  html { background: var(--hz-bg); color: var(--hz-fg); color-scheme: dark; }
  body {
    background: var(--hz-bg) !important;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Inter", "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
    font-size: 12.5px;
    line-height: 1.45;
    padding: 12px 14px !important;
    user-select: none;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  }

  .mono,
  .badge,
  .session-select,
  .b-val,
  .diag-impact,
  .group-pill,
  .chronic-badge,
  .retained-badge,
  .handoff-dot,
  .sig-val,
  .base-tok {
    font-family: ui-monospace, "JetBrains Mono", "Cascadia Code", Consolas, monospace;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  /* Strict WCAG Keyboard Focus States (shadcn/ui ring pattern) */
  button:focus-visible,
  select:focus-visible,
  input:focus-visible,
  summary:focus-visible,
  a:focus-visible,
  [tabindex]:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--ring) !important;
    outline-offset: 2px !important;
  }

  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *, *::before, *::after {
      animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
      animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
      transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
      scroll-behavior: auto !important;
    }
  }

  .wrap {
    max-width: 560px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 10px;
  }

  /* 1. Top Header */
  header {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    padding-bottom: 2px;
  }
  .title-group {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
  }
  .dot {
    width: 8px;
    height: 8px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: var(--hz-useful);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25);
  }
  .dot.warn { background: var(--hz-clutter); }
  .dot.danger { background: var(--hz-danger); }

  h1 {
    font-size: 14px;
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: -0.015em;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }

  .header-right {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
  }

  /* shadcn/ui Tabs Primitive for Simple / Advanced View Mode */
  .shadcn-tabs-list {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    background: var(--hz-card);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 2px;
    gap: 2px;
  }
  .shadcn-tab-trigger {
    background: transparent;
    border: none;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 2px 8px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background-color 0.15s ease, color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .shadcn-tab-trigger:hover {
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .shadcn-tab-trigger[aria-selected="true"] {
    background: var(--hz-card-alt);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
  }

  /* In Simple Mode, hide deep diagnostics & 40-cell timeline unless expanded */
  body[data-view-mode="simple"] .advanced-only {
    display: none !important;
  }

  .badge {
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 2px 9px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
    color: var(--hz-useful);
    border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .badge.warn {
    background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12);
    color: var(--hz-clutter);
    border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4);
  }
  .badge.danger {
    background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.14);
    color: var(--hz-danger);
    border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.45);
  }
  .icon-btn {
    background: var(--hz-card-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    height: 24px;
    padding: 0 8px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 500;
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 4px;
    transition: background-color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .icon-btn:hover {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }

  /* Conversation Switcher Bar */
  .session-bar {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    background: var(--hz-card);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 5px 10px;
  }
  .session-label {
    font-size: 11px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    font-weight: 600;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .session-select {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
    background: var(--hz-card-alt);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 3px 8px;
    font-size: 11.5px;
    font-weight: 500;
    font-family: inherit;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
  .session-select option {
    background: var(--hz-card);
    color: var(--hz-fg);
  }
  .prefix-lock-badge {
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 2px 6px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12);
    color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
    border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35);
    white-space: nowrap;
    cursor: help;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .prefix-lock-badge[hidden] {
    display: none !important;
  }

  /* 2. Storage Bar Card (Tufte High Data-Ink Ratio: Unified Horizontal Band) */
  .storage-card {
    background: var(--hz-card);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    border-radius: 8px;
    padding: 12px 14px;
  }
  .storage-top {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: 4px 10px;
    margin-bottom: 6px;
  }
  .used-big {
    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .used-of {
    font-size: 12px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    font-weight: 400;
    margin-left: 4px;
  }
  .free-label {
    font-size: 11.5px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
  }
  .delta-line {
    font-size: 11px;
    margin: 0;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    align-items: baseline;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  /* Turn-delta row: the file label ellipsizes; the token suffix never clips */
  .delta-line .delta-head { flex-shrink: 0; white-space: pre; }
  .delta-line .delta-src {
    flex: 0 1 auto;
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: 210px;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .delta-line .delta-tok { flex-shrink: 0; white-space: pre; }
  .delta-line.up { color: #cbd5e1 !important; }
  .delta-line.up.big { color: #f59e0b !important; }
  .delta-line.down { color: #10b981 !important; }
  .free-wrap { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; }
  .gear-btn {
    background: transparent !important;
    border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.3);
    border-radius: 4px;
    color: #cbd5e1 !important;
    font-size: 12px;
    line-height: 1;
    padding: 2px 5px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: border-color 0.15s ease, color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .gear-btn:hover { border-color: #f59e0b; color: #f59e0b !important; }
  .settings-pop {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 8px;
    margin: 0 0 10px;
    padding: 10px 12px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    background: var(--hz-bg) !important;
    font-size: 11.5px;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .settings-pop[hidden] { display: none; }
  .settings-pop label { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 10px; color: #e2e8f0 !important; }
  .settings-pop input {
    width: 110px;
    padding: 3px 6px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.4);
    background: #1e293b !important;
    color: #f8fafc !important;
    font-family: ui-monospace, Consolas, monospace;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    text-align: right;
  }
  .settings-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
  .settings-save, .settings-cancel {
    padding: 4px 12px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 600;
    cursor: pointer;
    border: 1px solid #f59e0b;
  }
  .settings-save { background: #f59e0b !important; color: #090d16 !important; }
  .settings-cancel { background: transparent !important; color: #cbd5e1 !important; border-color: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.4); }
  .settings-msg { font-size: 10.5px; color: #94a3b8 !important; }
  .settings-msg.err { color: #ef4444 !important; }
  .base-rows { list-style: none; margin: 6px 0 0; padding: 0; font-size: 10.5px; }
  .base-rows[hidden] { display: none; }
  .base-rows li { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; gap: 8px; padding: 3px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle); color: #cbd5e1 !important; }
  .base-rows li:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
  .base-rows .base-src { overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; text-align: left; }
  .base-rows .base-tok { flex: none; color: #f8fafc !important; text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .base-rows em { font-style: normal; color: #94a3b8 !important; font-size: 9.5px; }
  .chronic-badge { font-size: 9.5px; padding: 1px 6px; border-radius: 9px; border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.35); color: #cbd5e1 !important; white-space: nowrap; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .chronic-badge.hot { border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.6); color: #f59e0b !important; }
  .chip-btn.chronic { border-color: #f59e0b !important; color: #f59e0b !important; font-weight: 700; }
  .tip-dismiss { background: transparent !important; border: none; color: #94a3b8 !important; cursor: pointer; font-size: 11px; padding: 0 2px; }
  .tip-dismiss:hover { color: #f8fafc !important; }
  #sipTipCard a { color: #38bdf8 !important; }

  .bar-outer {
    position: relative;
    height: 18px;
    width: 100%;
    background: var(--hz-free);
    border-radius: 4px;
    margin-top: 18px;           /* reserved lane for the limit tag so it never overlaps the delta line */
    margin-bottom: 18px;        /* reserved lane below the track for the 50k Sync / ceiling tags */
  }
  .bar-track {
    display: flex;
    height: 100%;
    width: 100%;
    border-radius: 4px;
    overflow: hidden;
    gap: 1px;
  }
  .seg-useful { background: var(--hz-useful); height: 100%; transition: width 0.25s ease; }
  .seg-clutter { background: var(--hz-clutter); height: 100%; transition: width 0.25s ease, background-color 0.25s ease; }
  .seg-clutter.cached-warm { background: var(--hz-cached-history) !important; }
  .seg-clutter.cold-bloat { background: var(--hz-clutter) !important; }
  .seg-error { display: none; width: 0 !important; height: 100%; }

  .ceiling-clamp-pill {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 9.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 1px 6px;
    margin-left: 4px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.10);
    border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35);
    color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
    white-space: nowrap;
    vertical-align: middle;
  }
  .ceiling-clamp-pill[hidden] { display: none !important; }

  .cascade-line {
    position: absolute;
    left: 19.5%;
    top: -3px;
    bottom: -3px;
    border-left: 1px dashed rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.75);
    pointer-events: auto;
    cursor: help;
    z-index: 4;
  }
  .cascade-tag {
    /* Anchored BELOW the bar track so it can never collide with the Safe Limit tag above it */
    position: absolute;
    left: 3px;
    top: auto;
    bottom: -14px;
    font-size: 9.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #7dd3fc;
    white-space: nowrap;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  .hard-ceiling-line {
    position: absolute;
    right: 0;
    top: -3px;
    bottom: -3px;
    border-right: 2px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.75);
    pointer-events: auto;
    cursor: help;
    z-index: 4;
  }
  .hard-ceiling-tag {
    position: absolute;
    right: 2px;
    bottom: -13px;
    font-size: 9px;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #94a3b8;
    white-space: nowrap;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  .limit-line {
    position: absolute;
    left: 70%;
    top: -3px;
    bottom: -3px;
    border-left: 1px dashed rgba(251, 113, 133, 0.8); /* border-rose-400/80 */
    pointer-events: auto;
    cursor: help;
    z-index: 5;
  }
  .limit-line::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: -2.5px;
    top: -1px;
    width: 4px;
    height: 1px;
    background: rgba(251, 113, 133, 0.8);
  }
  .limit-tag {
    /* Right-aligned ABOVE the bar track, ending at the marker */
    position: absolute;
    left: -4px;
    right: auto;
    transform: translateX(-100%);
    top: -14px;
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #fda4af; /* text-rose-300 */
    white-space: nowrap;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  /* Flattened Horizontal Metric Band (No Nested Boxes-Within-Boxes) */
  .breakdown-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    gap: 0;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    padding-top: 10px;
  }
  .b-item {
    background: transparent;
    border: none;
    border-right: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
    border-radius: 0;
    padding: 0 10px;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .b-item:first-child { padding-left: 0; }
  .b-item:last-child { border-right: none; padding-right: 0; }
  .b-head {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    margin-bottom: 2px;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
  }
  .b-swatch {
    width: 7px;
    height: 7px;
    border-radius: 2px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .b-val {
    font-size: 14px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: -0.01em;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    white-space: nowrap;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .b-sub {
    font-size: 10.5px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    margin-top: 2px;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
  }

  /* 3. Action Center Card */
  .action-card {
    background: var(--hz-card);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    border-radius: 8px;
    padding: 10px 14px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 8px;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .action-card.warn {
    background: #191610 !important;
    border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .action-card.danger {
    background: #1e1216 !important;
    border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.4);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .action-msg {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 9px;
    font-size: 12px;
    line-height: 1.45;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .action-msg strong {
    color: #ffffff !important;
  }
  .action-icon {
    font-size: 14px;
    line-height: 1.2;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  /* Compact Guided Action Box - Flattened */
  .guided-step-box {
    background: transparent !important;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 0;
    padding: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 8px;
  }
  .action-card .action-icon, .action-card .action-msg { color: var(--hz-fg) !important; }
  .action-card.danger .guided-step-box {
    border: none;
  }

  .compact-instruction {
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    font-size: 11.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 1.5;
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
    border-left: 3px solid #f59e0b;
    background: var(--hz-bg) !important;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    cursor: default;
    user-select: text;
  }
  .compact-instruction kbd {
    font-family: ui-monospace, "JetBrains Mono", "Cascadia Code", Consolas, monospace;
    font-size: 11px;
    padding: 1px 6px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.4);
    background: #1e293b !important;
    color: #e2e8f0 !important;
  }
  .compact-instruction kbd.cmd-kbd {
    color: #f59e0b !important;
    border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.55);
    font-weight: 700;
  }
  .compact-reclaim {
    display: block;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: #f59e0b !important;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  /* While the green DEFER_WARM banner is visible, mute the urgent amber accent so hierarchy doesn't contradict it */
  #guidedStepBox[data-cache-advisory="warm-defer"] .compact-instruction {
    border-left-color: var(--hz-border);
  }

  .param-compact-details > summary {
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    user-select: none;
    padding: 2px 0;
  }
  .param-compact-details[open] > summary { margin-bottom: 4px; }
  /* Advanced mode: template is force-opened by applyViewMode(); hide the disclosure chrome */
  body[data-view-mode="advanced"] .param-compact-details > summary { display: none; }

  .param-compact-box {
    padding: 6px 10px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    border: 1px dashed var(--hz-border);
    background: rgba(15, 22, 35, 0.85);
    font-size: 10.5px;
    line-height: 1.45;
    color: var(--hz-fg-secondary) !important;
    user-select: text;
    word-break: break-word;
  }
  .param-compact-label {
    display: block;
    font-size: 9.5px;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
    margin-bottom: 2px;
  }
  .cache-compaction-advisory {
    padding: 6px 10px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 1.4;
  }
  .cache-compaction-advisory.warm-defer {
    background: rgba(13, 148, 136, 0.12);
    border: 1px solid rgba(13, 148, 136, 0.4);
    color: #5eead4 !important;
  }
  .cache-compaction-advisory.cold-compact-now {
    background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.14);
    border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45);
    color: #fde68a !important;
  }
  .artifact-drift-banner {
    padding: 6px 10px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 1.4;
    background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.14);
    border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.45);
    color: #fca5a5 !important;
  }
  .artifact-drift-banner[hidden] { display: none !important; }
  .baton-pass-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 6px 10px;
    padding: 6px 10px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35);
    background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.08);
  }
  .baton-pass-info {
    font-size: 10.5px;
    color: var(--hz-fg-secondary) !important;
    flex: 1 1 180px;
  }
  .baton-pass-info strong {
    color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
  }
  .btn-baton-pass {
    padding: 5px 11px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 700;
    cursor: pointer;
    border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.55);
    background: var(--hz-card-alt);
    color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
    white-space: nowrap;
    transition: background-color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .btn-baton-pass:hover {
    background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.16);
    border-color: var(--hz-anchor);
  }

  /* Collapsible Secondary Actions */
  .secondary-buttons {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 6px;
    margin-top: 8px;
  }
  .btn-secondary {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 135px;
    padding: 6px 10px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 600;
    cursor: pointer;
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    background: var(--hz-card-alt);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    text-align: center;
    transition: background-color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .btn-secondary:hover {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);
  }

  .feedback-toast {
    display: none;
    padding: 7px 10px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
    border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    font-size: 11px;
    line-height: 1.4;
  }

  /* 4. Diagnostic & Brain Playbook Cards (shadcn/ui Accordion / Collapsible) */
  .playbook-card {
    background: var(--hz-card);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    border-radius: 8px;
    overflow: hidden;
  }
  .playbook-header {
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
  }
  .playbook-title {
    font-size: 12px;
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: -0.01em;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .playbook-sub {
    font-size: 10.5px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .diag-item {
    padding: 0;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .diag-item:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
  .diag-item.diag-rollup summary.diag-top {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 7px 12px;
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    list-style: none;
    gap: 8px;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    transition: background-color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .diag-item.diag-rollup summary.diag-top::-webkit-details-marker { display: none; }
  .diag-item.diag-rollup summary.diag-top:hover {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.035);
  }
  .diag-item.diag-rollup .diag-body {
    padding: 6px 12px 10px 24px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 6px;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
  }
  .diag-top {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .diag-name {
    font-size: 11.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .diag-caret {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 8.5px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    transition: transform 0.15s ease;
    width: 10px;
  }
  .diag-item.diag-rollup[open] summary .diag-caret {
    transform: rotate(90deg);
  }
  .diag-impact {
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--hz-clutter);
    white-space: nowrap;
    text-align: right;
    min-width: 44px;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .diag-desc {
    font-size: 11px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    line-height: 1.45;
  }
  .diag-desc code {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    padding: 1px 4px;
    border-radius: 3px;
    color: var(--hz-anchor);
    font-family: ui-monospace, "JetBrains Mono", "Cascadia Code", Consolas, monospace;
    font-size: 10.5px;
  }
  .diag-actions {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 8px;
    margin-top: 3px;
  }
  .mini-btn {
    padding: 4px 9px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    font-weight: 600;
    cursor: pointer;
    background: var(--hz-card-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
    transition: background-color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .mini-btn:hover { background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12); border-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35); }
  .mini-btn.danger { color: var(--hz-danger) !important; }
  .mini-btn.danger:hover { background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12); border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35); }

  .diag-pill {
    font-size: 10px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
    padding: 2px 7px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
  }

  details.file-drawer summary {
    font-size: 10.5px;
    color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
    cursor: pointer;
    margin-top: 2px;
  }
  .file-sublist {
    margin-top: 4px;
    padding-left: 10px;
    border-left: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 4px;
  }
  .file-subrow {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  /* Diagnostic groups */
  .diag-group.reclaim { border-left: 2px solid var(--hz-clutter); }
  .diag-group.spike {
    border-left: 2px dashed var(--hz-muted);
    background: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.04);
  }
  .diag-group.tips { border-left: 2px solid var(--hz-anchor); }
  .group-head {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    padding: 6px 12px;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.025);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
  }
  .group-title { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.01em; color: #cbd5e1 !important; }
  .group-pill {
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 1px 7px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    white-space: nowrap;
    text-align: right;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .diag-group.reclaim .group-pill {
    display: none !important;
  }
  .group-meta {
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    white-space: nowrap;
    text-align: right;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .retained-badge {
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 2px 8px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    background: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.10);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
  }
  .prompt-btn.flash, .btn-secondary.flash {
    color: var(--hz-useful) !important;
    border-color: var(--hz-useful) !important;
  }

  /* 5. Optional Collapsible 40-Block Timeline */
  details.advanced-drawer {
    background: var(--hz-card);
    border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
    border-radius: 8px;
    padding: 8px 12px;
  }
  details.advanced-drawer summary {
    font-size: 11px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-weight: 600;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
  }
  details.advanced-drawer summary:hover {
    color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
  }
  .mini-timeline {
    display: flex;
    gap: 2px;
    height: 16px;
    margin-top: 10px;
    border-radius: 3px;
    overflow: visible;
  }
  .mini-cell { flex: 1; height: 100%; background: var(--hz-free); position: relative; border-radius: 1px; cursor: crosshair; }
  .mini-cell.effort-shift {
    border-top: 2px solid #f59e0b;
  }
  .effort-shift-badge {
    position: absolute;
    top: -9px;
    right: -2px;
    font-size: 8.5px;
    line-height: 1;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #fbbf24;
    background: var(--hz-bg);
    border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.65);
    border-radius: 3px;
    padding: 0 2px;
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 3;
  }
  .limit-reason-tip {
    display: inline-block;
    margin-left: 4px;
    font-size: 10px;
    color: var(--hz-anchor);
    cursor: help;
    vertical-align: baseline;
  }
  .high-effort-sludge-banner {
    background: #231518 !important;
    border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.55);
    border-left: 3px solid #ef4444;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 7px 10px;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 1.4;
    color: #fde68a !important;
  }
  .high-effort-sludge-banner[hidden] { display: none !important; }
  .mini-inspector { font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--hz-muted) !important; margin-top: 6px; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }

  .footer-meta {
    text-align: center;
    font-size: 10.5px;
    color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    padding-top: 2px;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
    /* ---- Progressive disclosure & guardrails ---- */
    .b-toggle { background: none; border: 0; padding: 0; margin: 0; text-align: left; font: inherit; font-size: 10.5px; cursor: pointer; color: var(--hz-muted) !important; }
    .b-toggle:hover { color: var(--hz-anchor) !important; }
    .b-toggle .caret { display: inline-block; font-size: 8.5px; transition: transform .15s; }
    .b-toggle[aria-expanded="true"] .caret { transform: rotate(90deg); }
    .signal-drawer { margin-top: 10px; border-top: 1px solid var(--hz-border); padding-top: 10px; background: transparent; }
    .signal-drawer[hidden] { display: none; }
    .sig-row + .sig-row { margin-top: 8px; padding-top: 8px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--hz-border-subtle); }
    .sig-head { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; gap: 8px; font-size: 11.5px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--hz-fg) !important; }
    .sig-val { color: var(--hz-useful); text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
    .sig-desc { font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--hz-muted) !important; margin-top: 2px; }
    .sig-desc code { font-family: ui-monospace, Consolas, monospace; color: var(--hz-anchor); }

    /* Single footer row under primary compact button */
    .action-footers-row {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      align-items: flex-start;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 6px 14px;
      margin-top: 4px;
      padding-top: 8px;
      border-top: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
    }
    .action-footers-row details {
      flex: 1 1 auto;
      font-size: 11px;
    }
    .action-footers-row details[open] {
      flex: 1 1 100%;
    }
    .action-footers-row details summary {
      cursor: pointer;
      color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
      font-size: 11px;
      font-weight: 600;
      list-style: none;
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 4px;
      user-select: none;
    }
    .action-footers-row details summary::-webkit-details-marker { display: none; }
    .action-footers-row details summary::before {
      content: '\25B6';
      display: inline-block;
      font-size: 8px;
      transition: transform 0.15s ease;
    }
    .action-footers-row details[open] summary::before {
      transform: rotate(90deg);
    }
    .action-footers-row details summary:hover {
      color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    }

    .survive-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 8px; margin-top: 8px; }
    .survive-col { border: 1px solid var(--hz-border); border-radius: 6px; padding: 6px 8px; font-size: 11px; }
    .survive-col.keep { background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.06); border-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.25); }
    .survive-col.drop { background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.06); border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25); }
    .survive-title { font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 3px; }
    .survive-col.keep .survive-title { color: var(--hz-useful); }
    .survive-col.drop .survive-title { color: var(--hz-clutter); }
    .survive-col ul { margin: 0; padding-left: 16px; color: var(--hz-muted) !important; }

    .handoff-dot { font-size: 10.5px; font-weight: 700; margin-left: 4px; }
    .handoff-dot.synced { color: var(--hz-useful); }
    .handoff-dot.stale { color: var(--hz-clutter); }
    .handoff-dot .task-sync-badge { color: inherit; }
    .handoff-status { margin-top: 8px; font-size: 11.5px; font-weight: 600; }
    .handoff-status[hidden], .handoff-confirm[hidden] { display: none; }
    .handoff-status.synced { color: var(--hz-useful); }
    .handoff-status.stale { color: var(--hz-clutter); }
    .btn-secondary.emphasis { border-color: var(--hz-clutter); }
    .handoff-confirm { margin-top: 8px; padding: 8px 10px; border: 1px solid var(--hz-clutter); border-radius: 6px; background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.08); font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--hz-fg) !important; }
    .handoff-confirm-btns { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 6px; }
    .mini-btn.muted { color: var(--hz-muted) !important; }

    /* Diagnostic item alignment & full-line drawers */
    .diag-drawer-full { margin: 0; }
    .diag-drawer-full summary {
      font-size: 11px;
      color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
      cursor: pointer;
      list-style: none;
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 5px;
      user-select: none;
    }
    .diag-drawer-full summary::-webkit-details-marker { display: none; }
    .diag-drawer-full summary::before { content: '\25B6'; font-size: 8px; }
    .diag-drawer-full[open] summary::before { content: '\25BC'; }
    .diag-footer-bar, .diag-actions-row {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: 8px;
      margin-top: 6px;
      padding-bottom: 2px;
    }
    .diag-actions-label {
      font-size: 10.5px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
    }
    .diag-actions-buttons {
      display: flex;
      gap: 6px;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      align-items: center;
    }
    .diag-hint {
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
      user-select: none;
      white-space: nowrap;
      align-self: center;
    }
    .prompt-btn.flash, .btn-secondary.flash, .chip-btn.flash {
      color: var(--hz-useful) !important;
      border-color: var(--hz-useful) !important;
    }
    .file-subrow.rich { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 4px 8px; }
    .file-subrow.rich .fpath { flex: 1 1 140px; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; text-align: left; color: #cbd5e1 !important; }
    .file-subrow.rich .fmeta { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; color: var(--hz-muted) !important; }
    .chip-btn { font: inherit; font-size: 10.5px; padding: 1px 7px; border-radius: 999px; border: 1px solid var(--hz-border); background: transparent; color: var(--hz-muted) !important; cursor: pointer; }
    .chip-btn:hover { color: var(--hz-anchor) !important; border-color: var(--hz-anchor); }
    .chip-btn.noisy { color: var(--hz-clutter) !important; border-color: var(--hz-clutter); }
    .payload-box { margin-top: 6px; padding: 6px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--hz-border); border-radius: 6px; font-size: 11px; color: var(--hz-fg) !important; word-break: break-all; }
    .payload-box .payload-kind { color: var(--hz-muted) !important; display: block; margin-bottom: 2px; }
    .payload-box .chip-btn, .payload-box .mini-btn { margin-top: 6px; }
    .quota-strip {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 4px 6px;
      padding: 5px 10px;
      background: var(--hz-card);
      border: 1px solid var(--hz-border);
      border-radius: 6px;
      font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Inter", "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
      font-size: 10px;
      letter-spacing: -0.01em;
      color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
      font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }
    .quota-strip[hidden] { display: none !important; }
    .quota-pills {
      display: flex;
      flex: 1 1 100%;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: 4px 8px;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
    }
    .quota-telemetry {
      display: inline-flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 4px;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
    .quota-Forecast { color: var(--hz-anchor) !important; font-weight: 600; text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
    .quota-Forecast[hidden] { display: none !important; }
    .quota-warn {
      color: var(--hz-clutter) !important;
      font-weight: 700;
      white-space: nowrap;
      margin-left: auto;
    }
    @keyframes hzTtlPulse {
      0%, 100% { border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35); box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(245, 158, 11, 0); }
      50% { border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.9); box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2); }
    }
    .quota-strip.ttl-pulse {
      animation: hzTtlPulse 1.6s ease-in-out infinite;
      border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.65);
    }
    .ttl-pulse-badge {
      color: var(--hz-clutter) !important;
      font-weight: 700;
      padding: 1px 5px;
      border-radius: 4px;
      background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.14);
      border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4);
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
    .prefix-bust-badge {
      color: var(--hz-danger) !important;
      font-weight: 700;
      padding: 1px 5px;
      border-radius: 4px;
      background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.14);
      border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.4);
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
    .quota-meta-row {
      display: flex;
      flex: 1 1 100%;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 4px 8px;
      font-size: 9.5px;
      color: var(--hz-fg-secondary) !important;
      border-top: 1px dashed var(--hz-border-subtle);
      padding-top: 3px;
      margin-top: 2px;
    }
    .quota-meta-row[hidden] { display: none !important; }
    .quota-credits {
      color: var(--hz-anchor) !important;
      font-weight: 600;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
    .overage-guardrail-banner {
      flex: 1 1 100%;
      margin-top: 3px;
      padding: 4px 8px;
      border-radius: 4px;
      font-size: 10px;
      font-weight: 600;
      line-height: 1.35;
    }
    .overage-guardrail-banner[hidden] { display: none !important; }
    .overage-guardrail-banner.danger {
      background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.14);
      border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.45);
      color: #fca5a5 !important;
    }
    .overage-guardrail-banner.warn {
      background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.14);
      border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45);
      color: #fde68a !important;
    }
    .bucket-bars { margin-top: 6px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
    .bucket-row { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 72px 1fr 64px; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--hz-muted) !important; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
    .bucket-row > span:first-child { text-align: left; color: #cbd5e1 !important; }
    .bucket-row > span:last-child { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
    .bucket-track { height: 5px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.06); border-radius: 3px; overflow: hidden; }
    .bucket-fill { height: 100%; background: var(--hz-anchor); border-radius: 3px; }
    .subagent-pills { margin-top: 8px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
    .subagent-pill { font-size: 10.5px; padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 5px; background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.08); border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25); color: var(--hz-fg) !important; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
    .mini-cell { cursor: pointer; position: relative; transition: transform 0.12s ease, opacity 0.12s ease, box-shadow 0.12s ease; }
    .mini-cell.low-attention-trough {
      outline: 1px dashed rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.65);
      box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.45);
    }
    .mini-cell.stranded-signal {
      outline: 1.5px solid #f59e0b;
      box-shadow: 0 0 6px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45);
    }
    .mini-cell.selected {
      outline: 2px solid #38bdf8;
      transform: translateY(-1px);
      z-index: 3;
    }
    .fracture-marker {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 10px;
      line-height: 1;
      color: #f59e0b !important;
      padding: 0 1px;
      user-select: none;
      cursor: help;
    }
    .stranded-signal-banner {
      margin-top: 6px;
      padding: 6px 8px;
      border-radius: 5px;
      font-size: 10.5px;
      line-height: 1.35;
      background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12);
      border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45);
      color: #fde68a !important;
    }
    .stranded-signal-banner[hidden] { display: none !important; }
    .cell-turn-filter-panel {
      margin-top: 6px;
      padding: 7px 9px;
      border-radius: 6px;
      background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.75);
      border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35);
      font-size: 10.5px;
      color: var(--hz-fg) !important;
    }
    .cell-turn-filter-panel[hidden] { display: none !important; }
    .cell-turn-filter-head {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 6px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: #38bdf8 !important;
      margin-bottom: 4px;
    }
    .host-watchdog-bar {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 8px;
      font-size: 10.5px;
      color: var(--hz-muted) !important;
      padding: 5px 12px;
      border-radius: 6px;
      background: var(--hz-card);
      border: 1px solid var(--hz-border-subtle);
      font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }
    .host-watchdog-bar.alert {
      border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.45);
      background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08);
      color: #fca5a5 !important;
    }
</style>
</head>
<body data-view-mode="simple">

<div class="wrap">
  <!-- 1. Header -->
  <header>
    <div class="title-group">
      <div class="dot" id="statusDot"></div>
      <h1>Context Memory</h1>
    </div>
    <div class="header-right">
      <span class="badge" id="statusBadge" hidden style="display:none !important;">Healthy</span>
      <span class="badge warn" id="confidenceBadge" hidden style="display:none;">Degraded</span>
      <div class="shadcn-tabs-list" role="tablist" aria-label="Telemetry density mode" data-slot="tabs-list">
        <button type="button" role="tab" class="shadcn-tab-trigger" id="tabSimple" data-view-tab="simple" aria-selected="true" title="Focused summary view">Simple</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" class="shadcn-tab-trigger" id="tabAdvanced" data-view-tab="advanced" aria-selected="false" title="Full diagnostic & timeline view">Advanced</button>
      </div>
      <button class="icon-btn" onclick="window.location.reload()" title="Reload panel" aria-label="Reload panel">&#x21bb;</button>
    </div>
  </header>

  <!-- Conversation Switcher Bar -->
  <div class="session-bar">
    <span class="session-label">Session</span>
    <select class="session-select" id="sessionSelect" aria-label="Select conversation" title="Select which conversation's context memory to inspect">
      <option value="auto">&#x26A1; Auto (Active Conversation)</option>
    </select>
    <span class="prefix-lock-badge mono" id="prefixLockBadge" hidden title="Active warm cache (&gt;50k tokens). Changing reasoning effort or switching models rewrites the system prefix and triggers a 100% uncached KV cache rebuild.">&#x1F512; Warm</span>
    <button class="icon-btn" id="btnOpenChat" title="Click: Switch IDE chat pane to the selected conversation | Alt+Click: Open local ~/.gemini/&lt;surface&gt;/brain/&lt;session-uuid&gt;/ folder in OS file explorer">Open &#x2197;</button>
  </div>

  <!-- Dual-Pool Quota & Cache-Read Telemetry Strip (Rank #1 + Phase 2 Layer 2) -->
  <div class="quota-strip mono flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap" id="quotaStrip" hidden>
    <div class="quota-pills flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap" id="quotaPills">5h Sprint: 0% &middot; 7d: 0% &middot; Cache: 0% hit (300s TTL)</div>
    <div class="quota-Forecast" id="quotaForecast" hidden></div>
    <div class="quota-meta-row mono" id="quotaMetaRow" hidden>
      <span id="quotaPoolReset"></span>
      <span class="quota-credits" id="quotaAiCredits" hidden></span>
    </div>
    <div class="overage-guardrail-banner" id="quotaOverageAlert" hidden></div>
  </div>

  <!-- 2. 200k/256k Token Bar & 3 Beginner-Friendly Breakdown Cards -->
  <section class="storage-card">
    <div class="storage-top">
      <div>
        <span class="used-big mono" id="usedBig">0k</span>
        <span class="used-of mono">/ <span id="usedMaxTok">200k</span> tokens (<span id="usedPct">0%</span>)</span>
        <span class="ceiling-clamp-pill mono" id="ceilingClampPill" hidden></span>
      </div>
      <div class="free-wrap">
        <div class="free-label mono" id="freeLabel">140k safe limit</div>
        <button class="gear-btn" id="btnSettings" type="button" title="Safe limit & reminder settings" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="settingsPop">&#x2699;</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <form class="settings-pop" id="settingsPop" hidden>
      <label>Safe limit (tokens)
        <input type="number" id="setSafeLimit" min="20000" max="1000000" step="1000" required>
      </label>
      <label>Remind me at (% of limit)
        <input type="number" id="setReminderPct" min="50" max="99" step="1" required>
      </label>
      <div class="settings-row">
        <button type="submit" class="settings-save">Save</button>
        <button type="button" class="settings-cancel" id="btnSettingsCancel">Cancel</button>
        <span class="settings-msg" id="settingsMsg"></span>
      </div>
    </form>
    <div class="delta-line mono" id="deltaLine" style="display:none;"></div>

    <div class="bar-outer">
      <div class="cascade-line" id="cascadeWatermarkLine" title="50k Cascade Sync Armed: Crossing 50k arms the automatic checkpointer. Completing a task.md item or switching subagents will trigger synchronous auto-compaction.">
        <span class="cascade-tag mono" id="cascadeWatermarkTag">50k Sync</span>
      </div>
      <div class="limit-line border-rose-400/80" id="limitLine" title="Attention Horizon (Safe Limit): Above this mark, earliest instructions start being forgotten">
        <span class="limit-tag text-rose-300" id="limitTag">Safe Limit</span>
      </div>
      <div class="hard-ceiling-line" id="hardCeilingLine" data-source="cortex/utils.GetCheckpointConfig" title="Hard assembly limit (cortex/utils.GetCheckpointConfig)">
        <span class="hard-ceiling-tag mono" id="hardCeilingTag">256k</span>
      </div>
      <div class="bar-track">
        <div class="seg-useful" id="barUseful" style="width: 0%" title="Active Signal"></div>
        <div class="seg-clutter" id="barClutter" style="width: 0%" title="Reclaimable"></div>
        <div class="seg-error" id="barError" style="width: 0%; display: none;" title="Resolved Errors"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="breakdown-grid">
      <div class="b-item" title="Rules, task plan, and active files the agent is working with right now">
        <div class="b-head">
          <span class="b-swatch" style="background:var(--hz-useful)"></span>
          <span>Active Signal</span>
        </div>
        <div class="b-val mono" id="valUseful" style="color:var(--hz-useful) !important;">0k (100%)</div>
        <button class="b-sub b-toggle" id="btnSignalToggle" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="signalDrawer" title="Click to see what is inside Active Signal" style="white-space:nowrap;"><span class="caret">&#x25B6;</span> Rules &amp; work</button>
      </div>

      <div class="b-item" title="Old conversation steps and duplicate file snapshots that can be safely freed">
        <div class="b-head">
          <span class="b-swatch" id="swatchClutter" style="background:var(--hz-clutter)"></span>
          <span id="lblClutter">Reclaimable</span>
        </div>
        <div class="b-val mono" id="valClutter">0k (0%)</div>
        <div class="b-sub" id="subClutter">Can be cleared</div>
      </div>

      <div class="b-item" title="Available memory before the agent begins truncating earliest instructions">
        <div class="b-head">
          <span class="b-swatch" id="swatchFree" style="background:var(--hz-free); border: 1px solid var(--hz-border)"></span>
          <span id="lblFree">Safe Headroom</span>
        </div>
        <div class="b-val mono text-slate-100" id="valFree" style="color:#f1f5f9 !important;">140k left</div>
        <div class="b-sub" id="subFree">Until 140k safe limit</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Active Signal drawer (progressive disclosure) -->
    <div class="signal-drawer" id="signalDrawer" hidden>
      <div class="sig-row">
        <div class="sig-head"><span class="sig-name">Workspace Baseline Overhead</span><span class="sig-val mono" id="sigBaseVal">0k (0%)</span></div>
        <div class="sig-desc">AGENTS.md / .agents/rules/, active skills &amp; loaded MCP tool schemas. Loaded every turn; <code>/compact</code> keeps it.</div>
        <div class="bucket-bars mono" id="bucketBars" hidden></div>
        <ul class="base-rows mono" id="baseRows" hidden></ul>
      </div>
      <div class="sig-row">
        <div class="sig-head"><span class="sig-name">Live Session Work</span><span class="sig-val mono" id="sigLiveVal">0k (0%)</span></div>
        <div class="sig-desc">Pinned task.md state &amp; your most recent active turns.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- 3. Smart Action Center: Step-by-Step Guided Action -->
  <section class="action-card" id="actionCard">
    <div class="action-msg">
      <span class="action-icon" id="actionIcon">&#x2713;</span>
      <div id="actionText">
        <strong>Memory is clean.</strong> Only active files and rules are loaded.
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Recommended Action Box (Shown when cleanup is recommended) -->
    <div class="guided-step-box" id="guidedStepBox" style="display:none;">
      <div class="high-effort-sludge-banner" id="highEffortSludgeBanner" hidden></div>
      <div class="artifact-drift-banner" id="artifactDriftBanner" hidden></div>
      <div class="cache-compaction-advisory warm-defer" id="cacheCompactionAdvisory" data-advisory-state="warm-defer">
        Cache is currently warm (95% token cost discount). Defer compaction until work pause to avoid 70s execution stall and complete KV cache invalidation.
      </div>
      <div class="compact-instruction" id="compactInstruction">
        Type <kbd class="cmd-kbd">/compact</kbd> in the chat box and press <kbd>Enter</kbd>
        <span class="compact-reclaim" id="compactReclaim"></span>
      </div>
      <details class="param-compact-details" id="parameterizedCompactDetails">
        <summary>Parameterized <code>/compact</code> template</summary>
        <div class="param-compact-box mono" id="parameterizedCompactBox">
          <span class="param-compact-label">Parameterized Compaction Template (Select &amp; Type in Chat)</span>
          <div id="parameterizedCompactText">/compact Focus on preserving: 1) Exported symbol signatures and state scales for [active workspace files], 2) Current checklist state in task.md.</div>
        </div>
      </details>
      <div class="baton-pass-row" id="batonPassRow">
        <div class="baton-pass-info">
          <strong>The Baton Pass (<code>/handoff</code>)</strong> &middot; Zero conversational debt &amp; 100% constraint retention
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="btn-baton-pass" id="btnGenerateHandoff" title="Writes docs/HANDOFF_<timestamp>.md summarizing active AST contracts, test states, and pending task.md tasks">Generate Handoff Markdown</button>
      </div>

      <div class="action-footers-row">
        <details class="survive-drawer" id="surviveDrawer">
          <summary>What survives /compact?</summary>
          <div class="survive-grid">
            <div class="survive-col keep">
              <div class="survive-title">&#x2713; Preserved</div>
              <ul>
                <li>AGENTS.md &amp; rules</li>
                <li>task.md state</li>
                <li id="surviveRecent">Most recent active turns</li>
              </ul>
            </div>
            <div class="survive-col drop">
              <div class="survive-title" id="surviveDropTitle">Compressed</div>
              <ul id="surviveDropList"></ul>
            </div>
          </div>
        </details>

        <details class="secondary-drawer" id="secondaryDrawer">
          <summary>Other actions (Save, refresh, start fresh) <span class="handoff-dot" id="handoffDot"></span></summary>
          <div class="secondary-buttons">
            <button class="btn-secondary" id="btnCheckpoint" title="Sends a prompt to the agent in this chat to save progress to task.md">&#x1F4AC; Ask Agent: Save task.md</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary" id="btnCondenseAst" title="Condenses stale duplicate file reads into compact AST signature stubs and collapses resolved stack traces (Prefix-Cache Safe)">&#x2702;&#xFE0F; Condense Stale Reads (AST Fold)</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary" id="btnNewChat" title="Opens a fresh chat session with handoff context from this conversation">&#x1F680; Resume in Clean Chat &#x2197;</button>
          </div>
          <div class="handoff-status" id="handoffStatus" hidden></div>
          <div class="handoff-confirm" id="handoffConfirm" hidden>
            <div id="handoffConfirmMsg"></div>
            <div class="handoff-confirm-btns">
              <button class="mini-btn" id="btnSyncFirst">Sync task.md first</button>
              <button class="mini-btn muted" id="btnStartAnyway">Start anyway</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </details>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="feedback-toast" id="feedbackToast"></div>
  </section>

  <!-- 4. Actionable Brain Diagnostics -->
  <section class="playbook-card advanced-only" data-slot="accordion">
    <div class="playbook-header">
      <span class="playbook-title">Why Memory Is Filling Up &amp; How to Fix It</span>
      <span class="playbook-sub" id="playbookSub">Live Brain Analysis</span>
    </div>
    <div id="playbookList"></div>
  </section>

  <!-- 5. Optional Collapsible 40-Turn Timeline -->
  <details class="advanced-drawer advanced-only" data-slot="collapsible">
    <summary><span>40-Cell Chrono-Attention Timeline</span><span class="mono" style="font-size:10px;">6.4k / cell</span></summary>
    <div class="mini-timeline" id="miniTimeline"></div>
    <div class="mini-inspector mono" id="miniInspector">Hover or click any block above to inspect 6.4k token slice</div>
    <div class="stranded-signal-banner" id="strandedSignalBanner" hidden></div>
    <div class="cell-turn-filter-panel mono" id="cellTurnFilterPanel" hidden></div>
    <div class="subagent-pills mono" id="subagentPills" hidden></div>
  </details>

  <!-- Host Runtime Watchdog Bar (Rank #3) -->
  <div class="host-watchdog-bar mono" id="hostWatchdogBar" hidden>
    <span id="hostWatchdogText">Host RAM: 0.0 GB &middot; Orphans: 0</span>
    <button class="mini-btn danger" id="btnReapOrphans" hidden title="Cleanly terminate detached language_server / MCP orphan processes">Reap Zombies</button>
  </div>

  <div class="footer-meta mono" id="footerMeta">Live updates active</div>
</div>

ter_assembler.py","key":"c:/projects/argon-test/studio/editorial/master_assembler.py","sessions":1,"totalReads":3,"windowSessions":18}],"delta":null,"workspaceName":"ga-visualizer-v2"}};
</script>

<script>
  let currentRenderedState = null;
  // Temporary diagnostic (?debug=1): confirms whether banner dimming is in-panel or host-side.
  let selectedSessionMode = 'auto';
  let latestAutoState = null;
  let currentWorkspaceBasename = '';

  function roundK(n) {
    if (!n || n <= 0) return 0;
    return Math.round((n / 1000) * 10) / 10;
  }

  function fmtKVal(k) {
    if (!k || k <= 0) return '0k';
    return (Math.round(k * 10) / 10).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k';
  }

  function fmtKFixed1(k) {
    if (!k || k <= 0) return '0.0k';
    return (Math.round(k * 10) / 10).toFixed(1) + 'k';
  }

  function fmtK(n) {
    if (!n || n <= 0) return '0k';
    return (n / 1000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k';
  }

  function formatShortPath(p) {
    if (!p) return 'unknown';
    const clean = p.replace(/\\/g, '/');
    const segs = clean.split('/').filter(Boolean);
    if (segs.length <= 2) return clean;
    return '.../' + segs.slice(-2).join('/');
  }

  // Hotfix: clean workspace-relative path for the /compact template (never `.../` prefixed).
  const WORKSPACE_TOP_LEVEL_DIRS = new Set(['src', 'tests', 'test', 'scripts', 'docs', 'lib', 'app', 'packages', 'config', 'sidecars', 'skills', 'rules', 'public', 'assets', 'bin', 'tools', 'examples']);
  function formatWorkspaceRelativePath(p) {
    if (!p) return '';
    const s = String(p).trim().replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^(\.\.\.\/)+/, '');
    const segs = s.split('/').filter(Boolean);
    if (!/^([a-zA-Z]:|\/)/.test(s)) return segs.join('/');
    const anchor = segs.findIndex((seg, i) => i < segs.length - 1 && WORKSPACE_TOP_LEVEL_DIRS.has(seg.toLowerCase()));
    return anchor >= 0 ? segs.slice(anchor).join('/') : segs.slice(-2).join('/');
  }

  // Hotfix: strict warmth — TTL > 0s alone is not a warm cache; require real cache reads.
  function isCacheTrulyWarmInline(cache) {
    return Boolean(
      cache &&
      Number(cache.ttlRemainingSec) > 0 &&
      Number(cache.cacheReadTokens) > 0 &&
      Number(cache.hitRatePct) > 0 &&
      !cache.prefixBustDetected
    );
  }

  // Hotfix: Tenth-of-a-Kilotoken parity — Active + Reclaimable always equals the headline total.
  // When `anchorReclaimTenths` is supplied (sum of rounded "Cleared by /compact" rows), Reclaimable is pinned
  // to it so the card, header pill, status badge and /compact subtitle agree, and Active absorbs the rounding.
  function computeDisplayTenthsParity(totalTokens, activeSignalTokens, anchorReclaimTenths) {
    const displayTotalTenths = Math.round(Math.max(0, Number(totalTokens) || 0) / 100);
    if (Number.isFinite(anchorReclaimTenths) && anchorReclaimTenths >= 0) {
      const displayReclaimableTenths = Math.min(displayTotalTenths, Math.round(anchorReclaimTenths));
      return { displayTotalTenths, displayActiveTenths: displayTotalTenths - displayReclaimableTenths, displayReclaimableTenths };
    }
    const displayActiveTenths = Math.min(displayTotalTenths, Math.round(Math.max(0, Number(activeSignalTokens) || 0) / 100));
    const displayReclaimableTenths = Math.max(0, displayTotalTenths - displayActiveTenths);
    return { displayTotalTenths, displayActiveTenths, displayReclaimableTenths };
  }
  function fmtTenths(t) {
    if (!t || t <= 0) return '0k';
    return (t / 10).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k';
  }

  function showToast(html) {
    const toast = document.getElementById('feedbackToast');
    toast.innerHTML = html;
    toast.style.display = 'block';
  }

  // ---- Prompt actions --------------------------------------------------------
  // Inside the IDE panel the sidecar bridge (/preload.js) can submit a message into the chat
  // directly. Outside the IDE (plain browser) it cannot, so the buttons fall back to copying.
  // Labels and icons always reflect which of the two will actually happen.
  let handoffState = null;
  const CAN_INJECT = typeof window.sidecar?.agent?.sendMessage === 'function' && window.parent !== window;

  const PROMPTS = {
    checkpoint: {
      short: 'Save task.md',
      text: 'Please save a self-contained checkpoint of our current progress, completed items, and remaining next steps to task.md so I can safely run /compact or start a clean session.'
    },
    lines: {
      short: 'Read line ranges',
      text: 'To keep context memory lean, please use StartLine/EndLine when reading large files instead of reading full files repeatedly.'
    },
    subagent: {
      short: 'Use subagent',
      text: 'For broad codebase searches or multi-file exploration, please spawn a research subagent instead of reading many files in this chat, and bring back only a short summary of what it finds.'
    },
    truncate: {
      short: 'Truncate Command Output',
      text: 'The last very large command/file output filled my context window. From now on, truncate command output (for example pipe through Select-Object -First 50 or use head/tail, grep, or line ranges) so a single step never injects more than a few thousand tokens.'
    },
    loop: {
      short: 'Break Loop & Re-Plan',
      text: 'Pause modifying files. Summarize the root cause of the recent errors in 3 bullet points and propose a clean verification plan before making the next edit.'
    },
    rule: {
      short: 'Save Rule to AGENTS.md',
      text: 'Review the corrections I gave you during this conversation and propose a concise rule we can add to the top 30% (Primacy zone) or bottom 30% (Recency zone) of AGENTS.md / .agents/CONTEXT.md (checking that total file length stays under the 200-line MEMORY.md truncation threshold).'
    }
  };

  function escAttr(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  }
  function promptLabel(key) {
    return (CAN_INJECT ? '\u26A1 Send to Agent: ' : '\uD83D\uDCCB Copy Prompt: ') + PROMPTS[key].short;
  }
  function promptTitleRaw(key) {
    const chat = currentRenderedState?.sessionTitle || 'the selected chat';
    return CAN_INJECT
      ? 'Sends this exact message into "' + chat + '" and submits it:\n\n' + PROMPTS[key].text
      : 'Copies this prompt to your clipboard. Paste it into the chat box and press Enter:\n\n' + PROMPTS[key].text;
  }
  function promptTitle(key) { return escAttr(promptTitleRaw(key)); }

  function ignorePromptText(path) {
    return 'Please add "' + path + '" to .antigravityignore in the workspace root so it is not read into context again, and tell me when it is done.';
  }
  function isIgnorablePath(p) {
    if (!p) return false;
    const s = String(p).replace(/\\/g, '/');
    if (/(^|\/)(package-lock\.json|yarn\.lock|pnpm-lock\.yaml|poetry\.lock|Cargo\.lock|Gemfile\.lock|composer\.lock|\.lock)$/i.test(s)) return true;
    if (/\.log$/i.test(s) || /(^|\/)logs?(\/|$)/i.test(s) || /\.jsonl$/i.test(s)) return true;
    if (/(^|\/)(dist|build|out|target|\.next|\.nuxt|coverage|node_modules)(\/|$)/i.test(s)) return true;
    if (/\.(min\.js|min\.css|map|pyc)$/i.test(s)) return true;
    if (/(^|\/)(scratch|\.scratch|tmp|temp)(\/|$)/i.test(s)) return true;
    return false;
  }
  function CAN_INJECT_LABEL_IGNORE(path) {
    return (CAN_INJECT ? 'Sends this message to the agent: ' : 'Copies this prompt (paste it into the chat box): ') + ignorePromptText(path);
  }

  // One delegated handler for data-act buttons rendered inside the playbook
  document.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-act]');
    if (b && b.dataset.act === 'ignore') {
      runPrompt('ignore', b, ignorePromptText(b.dataset.path));
      return;
    }
    if (b && b.dataset.act === 'dampen-hook') {
      try {
        flashButton(b, '&#x23F3; Registering hook...', 2000);
        const r = await fetch('/api/hooks/dampen', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            command: b.dataset.command || '',
            maxLines: 100,
            conversationId: currentRenderedState?.sessionId
          })
        });
        const data = await r.json();
        if (data && data.success) {
          flashButton(b, '&#x2713; Registered in .agents/hooks.json', 2500);
          showToast(`&#x2713; <strong>Registered fail-open PreToolUse CLI dampener</strong> in <code>.agents/hooks.json</code> (<code>head -n 100</code>).`);
        } else {
          showToast(`&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; ${escAttr(data?.error || 'Could not register CLI dampener hook.')}`);
        }
      } catch {
        showToast('&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Live daemon not reachable for hook registration.');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (b && b.dataset.act === 'promote-rule') {
      try {
        flashButton(b, '&#x23F3; Promoting rule...', 2000);
        const r = await fetch('/api/rules/promote', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            zone: b.dataset.zone || 'primacy',
            ruleText: b.dataset.rule || ''
          })
        });
        const data = await r.json();
        if (data && data.success) {
          flashButton(b, `&#x2713; Inserted at L${data.insertedAtLine} (${data.zoneUsed})`, 2500);
          const truncNote = data.truncationWarning ? ` &middot; <span style="color:var(--hz-clutter)">${escAttr(data.truncationWarning)}</span>` : '';
          showToast(`&#x2713; <strong>Promoted rule to ${escAttr(data.zoneUsed)} zone (line ${data.insertedAtLine}/${data.totalLinesAfter})</strong> in <code>${escAttr(formatShortPath(data.targetPath))}</code>${truncNote}`);
        } else {
          showToast(`&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; ${escAttr(data?.error || 'Could not promote rule.')}`);
        }
      } catch {
        showToast('&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Live daemon not reachable for rule promotion.');
      }
      return;
    }
    const toggle = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-toggle-sublist]');
    if (toggle) {
      const targetId = toggle.dataset.toggleSublist;
      const listEl = document.getElementById(targetId);
      if (listEl) {
        const nextOpen = listEl.hidden;
        listEl.hidden = !nextOpen;
        toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(nextOpen));
        const caret = toggle.querySelector('.caret');
        if (caret) caret.innerHTML = nextOpen ? '&#x25BC;' : '&#x25B6;';
      }
    }
  });

  function requestHostChatFocus() {
    const targetId = currentRenderedState?.sessionId || window.sidecar?.conversationId || null;
    try {
      if (window.parent && window.parent !== window) {
        window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'focusChatInput', command: 'antigravity.focusChatInput', conversationId: targetId }, '*');
      } else {
        window.postMessage({ type: 'focusChatInput', command: 'antigravity.focusChatInput', conversationId: targetId }, '*');
      }
    } catch {}
    try {
      if (typeof window.acquireVsCodeApi === 'function') {
        window._vscodeApi = window._vscodeApi || window.acquireVsCodeApi();
        window._vscodeApi.postMessage({ type: 'focusChatInput', command: 'antigravity.focusChatInput', conversationId: targetId });
      }
    } catch {}
    try { window.sidecar?.ui?.focusChatInput?.(targetId); } catch {}
    try { window.sidecar?.ui?.focusChat?.(targetId); } catch {}
  }

  // Temporarily swap a button's label for a confirmation, then restore it
  function flashButton(btn, html, ms) {
    if (!btn) return;
    if (btn.dataset.orig === undefined) btn.dataset.orig = btn.innerHTML;
    btn.innerHTML = html;
    btn.classList.add('flash');
    clearTimeout(btn._flashTimer);
    btn._flashTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      btn.innerHTML = btn.dataset.orig;
      btn.classList.remove('flash');
      delete btn.dataset.orig;
    }, ms || 2000);
  }

  async function runPrompt(key, btn, overrideText) {
    const p = { short: PROMPTS[key]?.short || '', text: overrideText || PROMPTS[key].text };
    const targetId = currentRenderedState?.sessionId || window.sidecar?.conversationId;
    const chat = currentRenderedState?.sessionTitle || 'the chat';

    if (CAN_INJECT) {
      try {
        await window.sidecar.agent.sendMessage(p.text, targetId);
        flashButton(btn, '\u2713 Sent to agent', 2000);
        showToast('&#x2713; <strong>Sent to "' + escAttr(chat) + '".</strong> Switch to that chat to see the reply.');
        return;
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('sidecar.agent.sendMessage failed, falling back to clipboard', err);
      }
    }
    try { await navigator.clipboard?.writeText(p.text); } catch {}
    requestHostChatFocus();
    flashButton(btn, '\u2713 Copied! Press Ctrl+V in chat', 2000);
    showToast(CAN_INJECT
      ? '&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Could not send automatically, so the prompt was <strong>copied</strong> instead. Press <strong>Ctrl+V</strong>, then <strong>Enter</strong> in chat.'
      : '&#x2713; <strong>Prompt copied.</strong> Press <strong>Ctrl+V</strong>, then <strong>Enter</strong> in chat.');
  }

  function stripTokPrefix(t) {
    return String(t || '').replace(/^(\s*\[[^\]]*\]\s*)+/, '');
  }

  const PREFIX_LOCK_WARNING_TEXT =
    'Active warm cache (>50k tokens). Changing reasoning effort or switching models rewrites the system prefix and triggers a 100% uncached KV cache rebuild.';

  function resolveSurfaceTag(surfaceOrPath) {
    const s = String(surfaceOrPath || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (s.includes('antigravity-cli') || s === 'cli' || s === '[cli]') return '[CLI]';
    if (s.includes('antigravity-ide') || s === 'ide' || s === '[ide]') return '[IDE]';
    return '[App]';
  }

  function evaluateCompositeSessionRiskInline(rawState, totalTokens, safeLimit) {
    const isTrulyWarm = isCacheTrulyWarmInline(rawState?.quota?.cache);
    if (rawState && rawState.sessionRisk && rawState.sessionRisk.band) {
      // Gate the precomputed warm badge on strict warmth too (guards stale/legacy state snapshots).
      if (rawState.sessionRisk.prefixLockWarning && !isTrulyWarm) {
        return { ...rawState.sessionRisk, isCacheTrulyWarm: false, prefixLockWarning: false, prefixLockCachedTokens: 0, prefixLockTooltip: '' };
      }
      return rawState.sessionRisk;
    }
    const q = rawState?.quota || {};
    const signals = rawState?.brainSignals || {};
    const hs = signals.activeSignal || {};
    const safeHorizon = Math.min(Number(safeLimit) > 0 ? Number(safeLimit) : 179000, 179000);
    const hasExplicitCache = Boolean(q.cache && typeof q.cache.ttlRemainingSec === 'number');
    const ttlSec = hasExplicitCache ? q.cache.ttlRemainingSec : 300;
    const cacheReadTokens = Number(q.cache?.cacheReadTokens || 0);
    const cacheHitPct = Number(q.cache?.hitRatePct || 0);
    const turnsBehind = typeof hs.taskMdTurnsBehind === 'number' ? hs.taskMdTurnsBehind : 0;
    const hasPendingTaskItems = Boolean(hs.hasPendingTaskItems);
    const w7Used = Number(q.weekly7d?.usedPct || 0);
    const w7Lockout = Boolean(q.weekly7d?.lockoutRisk);
    const overagesEnabled = Boolean(q.weekly7d?.aiCreditOveragesEnabled || rawState?.aiCreditOveragesEnabled);

    const isSafeHorizonBreached = totalTokens >= safeHorizon || totalTokens >= 179000 || Boolean(rawState?.amputation?.amputationCrisis || rawState?.telemetry?.amputationRisk);
    const is7dLockoutWithoutOverages = (w7Used >= 100 || w7Lockout) && !overagesEnabled;
    const isCacheExpired = ttlSec <= 0;
    const isCascadeArmedWithPendingTask = totalTokens > 50000 && hasPendingTaskItems;
    const isTaskMdStale = turnsBehind >= 1;

    const reasons = [];
    let band = 'GREEN';
    if (isSafeHorizonBreached || is7dLockoutWithoutOverages) {
      band = 'RED';
      if (isSafeHorizonBreached) reasons.push(`Safe horizon breached (${fmtK(totalTokens)} >= ${fmtK(safeHorizon)})`);
      if (is7dLockoutWithoutOverages) reasons.push('7d quota lockout risk active without AI Credit overages enabled');
    } else if (isCacheExpired || isCascadeArmedWithPendingTask || isTaskMdStale) {
      band = 'AMBER';
      if (isCacheExpired) reasons.push('Cache TTL == 0s (cold cache window expired)');
      if (isCascadeArmedWithPendingTask) reasons.push('Context > 50k with pending task.md checklist item (Cascade sync armed)');
      if (isTaskMdStale) reasons.push(`task.md is ${turnsBehind} turn${turnsBehind === 1 ? '' : 's'} stale`);
    } else {
      band = 'GREEN';
      reasons.push('Healthy (<179k tokens, warm KV cache TTL > 0s, task.md in sync)');
    }

    const effectiveCachedTokens = Math.max(
      cacheReadTokens,
      cacheHitPct > 0 ? Math.round(totalTokens * (cacheHitPct / 100)) : 0,
      !isCacheExpired ? totalTokens : 0
    );
    const prefixLockWarning = isTrulyWarm && effectiveCachedTokens > 50000;

    return {
      band,
      isCacheTrulyWarm: isTrulyWarm,
      dotClass: band === 'RED' ? 'dot danger' : (band === 'AMBER' ? 'dot warn' : 'dot'),
      reasons,
      summary: reasons.join(' \u00B7 '),
      prefixLockWarning,
      prefixLockCachedTokens: prefixLockWarning ? effectiveCachedTokens : 0,
      prefixLockTooltip: prefixLockWarning ? PREFIX_LOCK_WARNING_TEXT : ''
    };
  }

  const EFFORT_PROFILES = {
    HIGH: {
      tier: 'HIGH',
      safeLimit: 115000,
      thinkingReserve: 25000,
      label: 'High Effort',
      shortLabel: 'High',
      limitReason: '25k reserved for deep reasoning'
    },
    STANDARD: {
      tier: 'STANDARD',
      safeLimit: 140000,
      thinkingReserve: 0,
      label: 'Standard / Auto',
      shortLabel: 'Auto',
      limitReason: '140k recall ceiling'
    },
    FAST: {
      tier: 'FAST',
      safeLimit: 95000,
      thinkingReserve: 0,
      label: 'Fast / Low Effort',
      shortLabel: 'Fast',
      limitReason: 'Earlier middle-window dilution on fast tiers'
    }
  };

  function resolveEffortProfile(turnMetadata) {
    if (!turnMetadata) return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.STANDARD };
    if (typeof turnMetadata === 'object' && turnMetadata.tier && EFFORT_PROFILES[turnMetadata.tier]) {
      return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES[turnMetadata.tier], ...turnMetadata };
    }
    let effortStr = '';
    let modelStr = '';
    if (typeof turnMetadata === 'string') {
      const paren = turnMetadata.match(/\(([^)]+)\)/);
      if (paren) {
        effortStr = paren[1].trim();
        modelStr = turnMetadata.replace(paren[0], '').trim();
      } else {
        effortStr = turnMetadata.trim();
        modelStr = turnMetadata.trim();
      }
    } else if (typeof turnMetadata === 'object') {
      const rawTier = turnMetadata.tier || turnMetadata.effortTier || '';
      const rawEffort = turnMetadata.effort || turnMetadata.reasoningEffort || turnMetadata.reasoning_effort || '';
      const rawModel = typeof turnMetadata.model === 'string'
        ? turnMetadata.model
        : (turnMetadata.model?.id || turnMetadata.modelId || turnMetadata.model_id || turnMetadata.modelName || turnMetadata.rawLabel || '');
      const paren = String(rawModel).match(/\(([^)]+)\)/);
      effortStr = String(rawTier || rawEffort || (paren ? paren[1] : '')).trim();
      modelStr = String(rawModel).trim();
    }
    if (effortStr) {
      if (/\b(high|max|thinking|deep)\b/i.test(effortStr)) return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.HIGH };
      if (/\b(low|fast|lite)\b/i.test(effortStr)) return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.FAST };
      if (/\b(medium|standard|normal|auto|default|none)\b/i.test(effortStr)) return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.STANDARD };
    }
    const combined = `${effortStr} ${modelStr}`.trim();
    if (/\b(high|max|thinking|deep)\b|-high\b/i.test(combined)) return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.HIGH };
    if (/\b(low|fast|flash|lite)\b/i.test(combined)) return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.FAST };
    return { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.STANDARD };
  }

  function tierShortName(val) {
    const s = String(val || '').trim();
    if (/\b(high|max|thinking|deep)\b/i.test(s)) return 'High';
    if (/\blow\b/i.test(s)) return 'Low';
    if (/\b(fast|flash|lite)\b/i.test(s)) return 'Fast';
    if (/\bmed(ium)?\b/i.test(s)) return 'Medium';
    return 'Auto';
  }

  function formatEffortRouteLabel(stateObj) {
    if (!stateObj) return 'Auto';
    const transitions = stateObj.effortTransitions || stateObj.effortHistory || [];
    const activeProf = stateObj.activeEffortProfile ? resolveEffortProfile(stateObj.activeEffortProfile) : null;
    if (transitions.length > 0) {
      const first = transitions[0];
      const last = transitions[transitions.length - 1];
      const fromShort = tierShortName(first.fromLabel || first.fromTier || 'Auto');
      const toShort = tierShortName(activeProf?.shortLabel || activeProf?.tier || last.toLabel || last.toTier || 'Auto');
      if (fromShort !== toShort) {
        return `${fromShort} \u2192 ${toShort}`;
      }
      return toShort;
    }
    if (activeProf && activeProf.tier && activeProf.tier !== 'STANDARD') {
      return tierShortName(activeProf.shortLabel || activeProf.tier);
    }
    return 'Auto';
  }

  function populateSessionDropdown(activeId, recentSessions = [], liveTok = {}) {
    const sel = document.getElementById('sessionSelect');
    const catalog = window.__PRECOMPUTED_SESSIONS__ || {};
    // Live value wins; next is SQLite gen_metadata liveTokens; catalog snapshot is fallback
    const tokensFor = (sid, sEntry = null) => {
      if (liveTok[sid] != null) return liveTok[sid];
      if (sEntry && sEntry.liveTokens > 0) return sEntry.liveTokens;
      return catalog[sid]?.window?.totalActiveTokens || 0;
    };

    const sessions = (recentSessions && recentSessions.length > 0)
      ? recentSessions
      : Object.values(catalog).map(s => ({
          sessionId: s.sessionId,
          title: s.sessionTitle || s.sessionId.slice(0, 8),
          stepCount: s.window?.turnCount || 0,
          workspace: s.workspaceName || '',
          surface: s.storeSource || 'antigravity',
          surfaceTag: s.surfaceTag || resolveSurfaceTag(s.storeSource || s.logFilePath || 'antigravity')
        }));

    const autoState = latestAutoState || currentRenderedState;
    const autoId = (autoState && autoState.sessionId) || activeId;
    const autoMeta = sessions.find(s => s.sessionId === autoId);
    const autoTok = tokensFor(autoId, autoMeta);
    const autoSurfaceTag = autoMeta?.surfaceTag || autoState?.surfaceTag || resolveSurfaceTag(autoMeta?.surface || autoState?.storeSource || autoState?.logFilePath);
    const routeLabel = formatEffortRouteLabel(autoState);
    const fallbackTitle = stripTokPrefix(autoState?.sessionTitle || 'Active Conversation');
    const autoTitle = autoMeta
      ? `${autoSurfaceTag} [${autoTok ? fmtK(autoTok) : '?'} \u2022 ${routeLabel}] ${stripTokPrefix(autoMeta.title)}`
      : (autoTok ? `${autoSurfaceTag} [${fmtK(autoTok)} \u2022 ${routeLabel}] ${fallbackTitle}` : '\u26A1 Auto (Active Conversation)');

    let html = `<option value="auto" data-surface="${escAttr(autoMeta?.surface || autoState?.storeSource || 'antigravity')}">${autoTitle}</option>`;
    for (const s of sessions) {
      const tok = tokensFor(s.sessionId, s);
      const tokStr = tok ? fmtK(tok) : `${s.stepCount}t`;
      const sState = s.sessionId === currentRenderedState?.sessionId ? currentRenderedState : catalog[s.sessionId];
      const sRoute = sState && (sState.effortTransitions?.length || sState.activeEffortProfile) ? formatEffortRouteLabel(sState) : '';
      const badgeMid = sRoute && sRoute !== 'Auto' ? sRoute : (s.workspace || sRoute);
      const prefix = badgeMid ? `${tokStr} \u2022 ${badgeMid}` : tokStr;
      const sSurface = s.surface || sState?.storeSource || 'antigravity';
      const sTag = s.surfaceTag || sState?.surfaceTag || resolveSurfaceTag(sSurface);
```
