```html
      html += `<option value="${s.sessionId}" data-surface="${escAttr(sSurface)}">${sTag} [${prefix}] ${stripTokPrefix(s.title)}</option>`;
    }

    // Skip DOM rewrite when nothing changed so an open dropdown is not collapsed by the live refresh
    const sig = html + '|' + selectedSessionMode;
    if (sel.dataset.sig === sig) return;
    sel.dataset.sig = sig;
    sel.innerHTML = html;
    if (selectedSessionMode !== 'auto' && sessions.some(s => s.sessionId === selectedSessionMode)) {
      sel.value = selectedSessionMode;
    } else {
      sel.value = 'auto';
    }
  }

  // Tooling tip dismissal persists in localStorage (falls back to in-memory if storage is blocked)
  let sipTipHiddenMem = false;
  function sipTipDismissed() {
    try { return sipTipHiddenMem || localStorage.getItem('hz.sipTipDismissed') === '1'; } catch { return sipTipHiddenMem; }
  }
  function dismissSipTip() {
    sipTipHiddenMem = true;
    try { localStorage.setItem('hz.sipTipDismissed', '1'); } catch {}
    document.getElementById('sipTipCard')?.remove();
  }

  // Path key matching core/paths.mjs canonicalizePath (lowercase, forward slashes, no file:// prefix)
  function normPath(p) {
    return String(p || '').trim().replace(/^file:\/\/\/?/i, '').replace(/\\+/g, '/').replace(/^\/([a-zA-Z]:)/, '$1').replace(/\/+$/, '').toLowerCase();
  }

  // Dual-Watermark + Hard Ceiling markers on the context bar (Phase 3 Layer 3)
  function renderLimitLine(safeLimit, maxTokens, effortProfile = null) {
    const denom = Math.max(1, maxTokens || 256000);

    // Tick Mark 1 (50k tokens): Cascade Sync Armed
    const cascadeLine = document.getElementById('cascadeWatermarkLine');
    const cascadeTag = document.getElementById('cascadeWatermarkTag');
    if (cascadeLine && cascadeTag) {
      const cascadePct = Math.round(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (50000 / denom) * 100)) * 10) / 10;
      cascadeLine.style.left = cascadePct + '%';
      cascadeLine.dataset.tokens = '50000';
      cascadeTag.textContent = '50k Sync';
      cascadeLine.title = '50k Cascade Sync Armed: Crossing 50k arms the automatic checkpointer. Completing a task.md item or switching subagents will trigger synchronous auto-compaction.';
    }

    // Tick Mark 2 (70% of ceiling / configured safeLimit, 179k on 256k): Attention Horizon (Safe Limit)
    // Visible label is shortened ("179k Safe Limit"); the full text lives in the marker tooltip.
    const line = document.getElementById('limitLine');
    const tag = document.getElementById('limitTag');
    if (line && tag) {
      const rawPct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (safeLimit / denom) * 100));
      const pct = Math.round(rawPct * 10) / 10;
      line.style.left = pct + '%';
      line.dataset.tokens = String(safeLimit);
      const labelSuffix = effortProfile && effortProfile.label ? ` (${effortProfile.label})` : '';
      tag.textContent = `${fmtK(safeLimit)} Safe Limit (${(effortProfile && effortProfile.shortLabel) || 'Auto'})`;
      tag.style.whiteSpace = 'nowrap';
      const reasonSuffix = effortProfile && effortProfile.limitReason ? ` — ${effortProfile.limitReason}` : '';
      line.title = `${fmtK(safeLimit)} Attention Horizon (Safe Limit)${labelSuffix}${reasonSuffix}: Above this mark, earliest instructions start being forgotten`;
    }

    // Tick Mark 3 (Ceiling / 256k): Hard assembly limit (cortex/utils.GetCheckpointConfig)
    const ceilingLine = document.getElementById('hardCeilingLine');
    const ceilingTag = document.getElementById('hardCeilingTag');
    if (ceilingLine && ceilingTag) {
      ceilingLine.dataset.tokens = String(denom);
      ceilingLine.dataset.source = 'cortex/utils.GetCheckpointConfig';
      ceilingTag.textContent = fmtK(denom);
      ceilingLine.title = `${fmtK(denom)} Hard assembly limit (cortex/utils.GetCheckpointConfig)`;
    }
  }

  // Settings popover (safe limit + reminder %), saved through the daemon's /api/settings
  (function initSettings() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnSettings');
    const pop = document.getElementById('settingsPop');
    const inLimit = document.getElementById('setSafeLimit');
    const inPct = document.getElementById('setReminderPct');
    const msg = document.getElementById('settingsMsg');
    if (!btn || !pop) return;
    const setMsg = (t, err) => { msg.textContent = t; msg.className = 'settings-msg' + (err ? ' err' : ''); };
    const close = () => { pop.hidden = true; btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); };
    btn.onclick = async () => {
      if (!pop.hidden) { close(); return; }
      const w = currentRenderedState?.window || {};
      inLimit.value = w.safeLimit || 140000;
      inPct.value = w.safeLimit && w.reminderAt ? Math.round((w.reminderAt / w.safeLimit) * 100) : 85;
      setMsg('');
      pop.hidden = false;
      btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
      try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/settings');
        if (r.ok) {
          const { settings } = await r.json();
          if (settings) { inLimit.value = settings.safeLimitTokens; inPct.value = settings.reminderAtPct; }
        }
      } catch { setMsg('Live daemon not reachable; saving is unavailable.', true); }
    };
    document.getElementById('btnSettingsCancel').onclick = close;
    pop.onsubmit = async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setMsg('Saving\u2026');
      try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/settings', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ safeLimitTokens: Number(inLimit.value), reminderAtPct: Number(inPct.value) })
        });
        const data = await r.json();
        if (!r.ok || !data.success) throw new Error(data.error || ('HTTP ' + r.status));
        inLimit.value = data.settings.safeLimitTokens;
        inPct.value = data.settings.reminderAtPct;
        if (data.state) renderState(data.state);
        setMsg(`Saved: ${fmtK(data.settings.safeLimitTokens)} limit, reminder at ${data.settings.reminderAtPct}%`);
        setTimeout(close, 1200);
      } catch (err) {
        setMsg('Could not save: ' + (err?.message || err), true);
      }
    };
  })();

  // Per-source baseline rows + 5-Bucket Zero-Turn Customization bars (Rank #5)
  function renderBaselineRows(baseline, measuredBaseTok) {
    const ul = document.getElementById('baseRows');
    const bucketContainer = document.getElementById('bucketBars');
    if (bucketContainer) {
      const buckets = baseline && baseline.buckets ? Object.values(baseline.buckets) : [];
      if (buckets.length > 0) {
        bucketContainer.hidden = false;
        bucketContainer.innerHTML = buckets.map(b => `
          <div class="bucket-row" title="${escAttr(b.label)}: ${fmtK(b.tokens)} / ${fmtK(b.budgetTokens)} (${b.usedPct}%)">
            <span>${escAttr(b.label.split(' ')[0])}</span>
            <div class="bucket-track"><div class="bucket-fill" style="width:${Math.min(100, b.usedPct)}%;background:${b.usedPct >= 60 ? 'var(--hz-clutter)' : 'var(--hz-anchor)'}"></div></div>
            <span>${fmtK(b.tokens)} / 20k</span>
          </div>
        `).join('');
      } else {
        bucketContainer.hidden = true;
        bucketContainer.innerHTML = '';
      }
    }
    if (!ul) return;
    const rows = baseline && Array.isArray(baseline.rows) ? baseline.rows : [];
    if (!rows.length) { ul.hidden = true; ul.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    const skills = rows.filter(r => r.kind === 'skill');
    const items = rows.filter(r => r.kind !== 'skill').map(r => ({
      label: r.kind === 'mcp' && r.toolCount ? `${r.source} (${r.toolCount} tools)` : r.source,
      tokens: r.tokens,
      title: r.kind === 'mcp' ? `Tool list ~${fmtK(r.tokens)}; full schemas ~${fmtK(r.schemaTokens || 0)} if loaded eagerly` : (r.path || '')
    }));
    if (skills.length) {
      items.push({ label: `Skill list (${skills.length} skill${skills.length === 1 ? '' : 's'})`, tokens: skills.reduce((s, r) => s + r.tokens, 0),
        title: skills.map(r => r.source.replace(/^skill: /, '')).join(', ') });
    }
    items.sort((a, b) => b.tokens - a.tokens);
    const shown = items.slice(0, 8);
    const rest = items.slice(8).reduce((s, r) => s + r.tokens, 0);
    if (rest > 0) shown.push({ label: `${items.length - 8} more sources`, tokens: rest, title: '' });
    const estTotal = items.reduce((s, r) => s + r.tokens, 0);
    if (measuredBaseTok > estTotal + 500) {
      shown.push({ label: 'Unattributed (system prompt, tool defs, older turns)', tokens: measuredBaseTok - estTotal, title: 'Baseline overhead not explained by the files above', unattributed: true });
    }
    const html = shown.map(r =>
      `<li title="${escAttr(r.title || '')}"><span class="base-src">${escAttr(r.label)}</span><span class="base-tok">${fmtK(r.tokens)}${r.unattributed ? '' : ' <em>est.</em>'}</span></li>`).join('');
    if (ul.dataset.sig !== html) { ul.innerHTML = html; ul.dataset.sig = html; }
    ul.hidden = false;
  }

  // Turn-to-turn delta line (hidden when there is no previous-turn snapshot)
  function renderDelta(delta) {
    const el = document.getElementById('deltaLine');
    if (!el) return;
    if (!delta || typeof delta.tokens !== 'number' || Math.abs(delta.tokens) < 100) {
      el.style.display = 'none';
      el.textContent = '';
      return;
    }
    el.style.display = 'flex';
    if (delta.tokens < 0) {
      el.className = 'delta-line mono down';
      const suffix = delta.rewound ? ' (rewound step)' : (delta.compacted ? ' (compacted)' : '');
      el.textContent = `\u25BC \u2212${fmtK(-delta.tokens)} since last turn${suffix}`;
      return;
    }
    el.className = 'delta-line mono up' + (delta.tokens > 5000 ? ' big' : '');
    // Flex row: [head (fixed)] [source label (ellipsizes)] [token suffix (never clipped)]
    el.textContent = '';
    const head = document.createElement('span');
    head.className = 'delta-head';
    el.appendChild(head);
    if (delta.topSource && delta.topSource.label) {
      head.textContent = `\u25B2 +${fmtK(delta.tokens)} since last turn \u00B7 largest: `;
      // Never surface a raw "generic"/empty label: map to a human-readable source
      let srcLabel = String(delta.topSource.label).trim();
      if (!srcLabel || /^(generic|unknown|model output)$/i.test(srcLabel)) srcLabel = 'system context';
      else if (/^(planner response|model response)$/i.test(srcLabel)) srcLabel = 'agent response';
      else if (/^(your message|user input|user message)$/i.test(srcLabel)) srcLabel = 'user prompt';
      const srcEl = document.createElement('span');
      srcEl.className = 'delta-src';
      srcEl.textContent = middleTruncateFilenames(srcLabel, 24);
      srcEl.title = srcLabel;
      const tokEl = document.createElement('span');
      tokEl.className = 'delta-tok';
      tokEl.textContent = ` (~${fmtK(delta.topSource.tokens)})`;
      el.appendChild(srcEl);
      el.appendChild(tokEl);
    } else {
      head.textContent = `\u25B2 +${fmtK(delta.tokens)} since last turn`;
    }
    el.title = 'Change in context size since the end of your previous message';
  }

  // Middle-truncates any whitespace-delimited token longer than `max` chars
  // (e.g. verify_e2e_context_memory.mjs -> verify_e2e...memory.mjs), keeping the extension visible.
  function middleTruncateFilenames(label, max = 24) {
    return String(label || '').split(' ').map(tok => {
      if (tok.length <= max) return tok;
      const base = tok.replace(/\\/g, '/').split('/').pop();
      if (base.length <= max) return base;
      const tailLen = Math.max(8, Math.floor((max - 3) / 2));
      const headLen = Math.max(4, max - 3 - tailLen);
      return base.slice(0, headLen).replace(/[_\-.]+$/, '') + '...' + base.slice(-tailLen);
    }).join(' ');
  }

  function renderState(raw, fromCatalog = false) {
    if (!raw || (!raw.window && !raw.telemetry && !raw.cells)) return;
    if (!fromCatalog) {
      latestAutoState = raw;
      if (selectedSessionMode !== 'auto') {
        const catMatch = (window.__PRECOMPUTED_SESSIONS__ || {})[selectedSessionMode];
        if (catMatch && raw.sessionId !== selectedSessionMode) {
          raw = catMatch;
        }
      } else if (currentWorkspaceBasename && raw.workspace?.canonicalPath) {
        const rawWs = raw.workspace.canonicalPath.split('/').pop();
        if (rawWs && rawWs.toLowerCase() !== currentWorkspaceBasename.toLowerCase()) {
          const matchByWs = Object.values(window.__PRECOMPUTED_SESSIONS__ || {}).find(
            s => (s.workspaceName || '').toLowerCase() === currentWorkspaceBasename.toLowerCase()
          );
          if (matchByWs) raw = matchByWs;
        }
      }
    }
    currentRenderedState = raw;

    const isV3 = Boolean(raw.telemetry && !raw.metrics);
    const telemetry = raw.telemetry || {};
    const metrics = raw.metrics || {};
    const win = raw.window || {};
    const sessionMeta = raw.sessionMeta || {};
    const amputation = raw.amputation || raw.flags || {};
    const cells = raw.cells || [];
    const breakdown = raw.breakdown || raw.tokenBreakdown || {};
    const signals = raw.brainSignals || {};


    // 1. Token Totals (Dynamic 200k / 256k / 512k / 900k / 1M Ceiling)
    const modelIdLower = String(raw.model?.id || sessionMeta.activeModel || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const isClaude46Model = modelIdLower.includes('claude-sonnet') || modelIdLower.includes('claude-opus-4-6') || modelIdLower.includes('claude-opus-4.6') || modelIdLower.includes('claude-3-5-sonnet');
    const maxTokens = win.maxTokens || (raw.model?.nativeCeilingTokens) || (isClaude46Model ? 200000 : 200000);
    const totalTokens = isV3 ? (telemetry.totalInputTokens || 0) : (win.totalActiveTokens || 0);
    const usedPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((totalTokens / maxTokens) * 100));
    const maxTokEl = document.getElementById('usedMaxTok');
    if (maxTokEl) maxTokEl.textContent = fmtK(maxTokens);

    const clampPillEl = document.getElementById('ceilingClampPill');
    if (clampPillEl) {
      const isArgonClamped = Boolean(
        raw.model?.isClamped1MModel ||
        ((modelIdLower.includes('argon') || modelIdLower.includes('gemini-4')) && maxTokens === 256000)
      );
      const isPatchedCli = Boolean(
        raw.model?.ceilingSource === 'patched_cli' ||
        raw.model?.patchedTier ||
        maxTokens === 512000 ||
        maxTokens === 900000
      );
      if (isArgonClamped) {
        clampPillEl.hidden = false;
        clampPillEl.textContent = raw.model?.clampedLabel || '256k Clamped (1M Model Capped by Cortex 0x3E800)';
        clampPillEl.title = 'Gemini 4 Argon has a 1M native context window but is capped at 256k (0x3E800) by unpatched Cortex GetCheckpointConfig.';
      } else if (isPatchedCli) {
        clampPillEl.hidden = false;
        clampPillEl.textContent = `${fmtK(maxTokens)} Patched CLI`;
        clampPillEl.title = `Patched CLI context ceiling override active (${fmtK(maxTokens)} tokens).`;
      } else {
        clampPillEl.hidden = true;
        clampPillEl.textContent = '';
      }
    }

    // Dropdown badges are bound to the same live token value as the header counter
    const liveTok = { [raw.sessionId]: totalTokens };
    if (latestAutoState && latestAutoState.sessionId && latestAutoState.sessionId !== raw.sessionId) {
      liveTok[latestAutoState.sessionId] = latestAutoState.window?.totalActiveTokens || 0;
    }
    populateSessionDropdown(raw.sessionId, raw.recentSessions, liveTok);

    // Keep selected option label in #sessionSelect strictly synchronized with live state
    const selEl = document.getElementById('sessionSelect');
    if (selEl && selEl.selectedOptions && selEl.selectedOptions[0]) {
      const activeOpt = selEl.selectedOptions[0];
      const liveRoute = formatEffortRouteLabel(raw);
      const liveTag = raw.surfaceTag || resolveSurfaceTag(raw.storeSource || raw.logFilePath || activeOpt.dataset?.surface);
      const liveTitle = stripTokPrefix(raw.sessionTitle || activeOpt.textContent || 'Conversation');
      activeOpt.textContent = `${liveTag} [${fmtK(totalTokens)} \u2022 ${liveRoute}] ${liveTitle}`;
    }

    const clutterTokens = isV3
      ? (telemetry.sludgeTokens || 0)
      : ((breakdown.STALE_DUPLICATE || 0) + (breakdown.DEAD_ERROR || 0) + (breakdown.FOREIGN_BLEED || 0));

    const usefulTokens = Math.max(0, totalTokens - clutterTokens);
    const purePct = totalTokens > 0 ? (usefulTokens / totalTokens) * 100 : 100;
    const pureRound = Math.round(purePct);
    const clutterPct = totalTokens > 0 ? Math.round((clutterTokens / totalTokens) * 100) : 0;
    const errorTokens = breakdown.DEAD_ERROR || 0;
    const staleOnlyTokens = Math.max(0, clutterTokens - errorTokens);

    // Precompute visible diagnostic rows for "Cleared by /compact" so that rounded row k-values
    // sum identically to the header pill, Reclaimable card, and /compact button label.
    const rawOffenders = raw.offenders || [];
    const dupFilesRaw = signals.duplicateReads?.topFiles || rawOffenders
      .filter(o => !String(o.target).startsWith('Conversational Turn') && o.target !== 'Older chat history (>25 turns ago)' && !String(o.category).includes('ERROR'))
      .map(o => ({ path: o.target, occurrences: o.occurrences || 1, wastedTokens: o.wastedTokens || 0 }));
    // Cross-session repeat offenders: files re-read in >= 3 recent sessions float to the top
    const chronicMap = new Map((raw.chronicReads || []).map(c => [normPath(c.path), c]));
    const chronicOf = f => chronicMap.get(normPath(f.path));
    const isChronic = f => (chronicOf(f)?.sessions || 0) >= 3;
    const dupFiles = dupFilesRaw.slice().sort((a, b) => (isChronic(b) ? 1 : 0) - (isChronic(a) ? 1 : 0));
    const dupTotalTok = signals.duplicateReads?.reclaimableTokens ?? dupFiles.reduce((s, f) => s + f.wastedTokens, 0);

    const oldTurnOff = rawOffenders.find(o => o.target === 'Older chat history (>25 turns ago)' || String(o.target).startsWith('Conversational Turn'));
    const oldTurnCount = signals.oldHistory?.turnCount ?? (oldTurnOff ? oldTurnOff.occurrences : 0);
    const oldTurnTok = signals.oldHistory?.reclaimableTokens ?? (oldTurnOff ? oldTurnOff.wastedTokens : 0);

    const isErrLoop = Boolean(signals.deadErrors?.isErrorLoop);
    const errTok = signals.deadErrors?.reclaimableTokens || errorTokens;

    const hasDupRow = dupFiles.length > 0 && dupTotalTok > 0;
    const hasOldTurnRow = oldTurnCount > 0 && oldTurnTok > 0;
    const hasErrRow = isErrLoop || errTok > 0;

    const dupRowK = hasDupRow ? roundK(dupTotalTok) : 0;
    const oldTurnRowK = hasOldTurnRow ? roundK(oldTurnTok) : 0;
    const errRowK = hasErrRow ? roundK(errTok) : 0;

    const hasVisibleReclaimRows = hasDupRow || hasOldTurnRow || hasErrRow;
    const reclaimSumK = hasVisibleReclaimRows
      ? Math.round((dupRowK + oldTurnRowK + errRowK) * 10) / 10
      : roundK(clutterTokens);
    const reclaimSumStr = fmtKVal(reclaimSumK);

    // 2. Top Storage Header & Bar Widths (Effort-Tier Aware)
    const transitions = raw.effortTransitions || raw.effortHistory || [];
    const hasEffortMeta = Boolean(
      raw.activeEffortProfile || raw.effort || raw.effortTier || raw.turnMetadata || transitions.length > 0
    );
    const effortProfile = raw.activeEffortProfile
      ? resolveEffortProfile(raw.activeEffortProfile)
      : (hasEffortMeta
          ? resolveEffortProfile(raw.turnMetadata || raw.effort || raw.effortTier || transitions[transitions.length - 1]?.toTier)
          : { ...EFFORT_PROFILES.STANDARD });

    // 2b. Dual-Pool Quota, Reset Timers, Overage Guardrails & Cache-Read Telemetry Strip (Phase 2 Layer 2)
    const quotaStrip = document.getElementById('quotaStrip');
    const quotaPills = document.getElementById('quotaPills');
    const quotaForecast = document.getElementById('quotaForecast');
    const quotaMetaRow = document.getElementById('quotaMetaRow');
    const quotaPoolReset = document.getElementById('quotaPoolReset');
    const quotaAiCredits = document.getElementById('quotaAiCredits');
    const quotaOverageAlert = document.getElementById('quotaOverageAlert');
    if (quotaStrip && raw.quota) {
      const q = raw.quota;
      const s5 = q.sprint5h?.usedPct ?? 0;
      const w7 = q.weekly7d?.usedPct ?? 0;
      const cHit = q.cache?.hitRatePct ?? 0;
      const ttlSec = q.cache?.ttlRemainingSec ?? 300;
      const isPrefixBust = Boolean(
        ttlSec > 0 &&
        !q.cache?.isCacheCold &&
        (
          q.cache?.prefixBustDetected ||
          (q.cache && q.cache.cacheReadTokens === 0 && (q.cache.uncachedInputTokens > 0 || cHit === 0) && typeof q.cache.idleSeconds === 'number' && q.cache.idleSeconds > 0 && q.cache.idleSeconds < 300 && q.cache.prefixBustDetected !== false && q.cache.explicitZeroCacheRead === true)
        )
      );
      const isTtlPulse = Boolean(
        q.cache?.ttlExpirationPulse ||
        (!isPrefixBust && ttlSec > 0 && ttlSec <= 45)
      );

      quotaStrip.classList.toggle('ttl-pulse', isTtlPulse);
      quotaStrip.dataset.ttlPulse = String(isTtlPulse);
      quotaStrip.dataset.prefixBust = String(isPrefixBust);

      const pulseBadge = isTtlPulse
        ? ` <span class="ttl-pulse-badge" id="ttlPulseBadge" title="Warm prefix cache expires in ${ttlSec}s! Send your next prompt or compact now before losing warm KV cache state.">&#x23F1; ${ttlSec}s left</span>`
        : '';
      const cacheInline = isPrefixBust
        ? `Cache: <span class="prefix-bust-badge" id="prefixBustBadge" title="cache_read_tokens dropped to 0 within the 300s window">&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Prefix Bust (system_message / config drift)</span>`
        : `Cache: <strong>${cHit}%</strong> hit (${ttlSec}s TTL)${pulseBadge}`;
      const lockoutWarn = q.weekly7d?.lockoutRisk ? '<span class="quota-warn" title="7-Day Weekly pool is depleting faster than 5h Sprint">&#x26A0; 7d Lockout Risk</span>' : '';
      quotaStrip.hidden = false;
      const s5WarnCls = s5 >= 99 ? ' style="color:var(--hz-danger) !important;font-weight:700;"' : (s5 >= 85 ? ' style="color:var(--hz-clutter) !important;font-weight:700;"' : '');
      const w7WarnCls = w7 >= 99 ? ' style="color:var(--hz-danger) !important;font-weight:700;"' : (w7 >= 85 ? ' style="color:var(--hz-clutter) !important;font-weight:700;"' : '');
      quotaPills.innerHTML = `<span class="quota-telemetry">5h Sprint: <strong${s5WarnCls}>${s5}%</strong> &middot; 7d: <strong${w7WarnCls}>${w7}%</strong> &middot; ${cacheInline}</span>${lockoutWarn}`;
      const isHighForecast = Boolean(effortProfile?.tier === 'HIGH' && q.forecast?.nextTurnHighSprintPct);
      quotaForecast.textContent = isHighForecast
        ? `Next High turn: ~${q.forecast.nextTurnHighSprintPct}% of 5h`
        : (isPrefixBust ? '\u26A0\uFE0F Prefix Bust (system_message / config drift)' : `Cache: ${cHit}% hit (${ttlSec}s TTL)`);
      quotaForecast.hidden = !isHighForecast;

      // Pool-specific reset timers & Google One AI Credits
      const s5PoolLabel = q.sprint5h?.poolCountdownLabel || (q.sprint5h?.resetLabel ? `${q.sprint5h.modelPoolName || 'Gemini 4 Argon'}: Resets in ${q.sprint5h.resetLabel}` : '');
      const w7PoolLabel = q.weekly7d?.poolCountdownLabel || (q.weekly7d?.resetLabel ? `7d Usage: ${w7}% (Resets in ${q.weekly7d.resetLabel})` : '');
      const creditsBalance = q.aiCredits?.balance ?? (typeof q.aiCredits === 'number' ? q.aiCredits : null);
      const creditsLabel = q.aiCredits?.label || (creditsBalance !== null && creditsBalance !== undefined ? `\u2726 AI Credits: ${Number(creditsBalance).toLocaleString('en-US')}` : '');

      if (quotaMetaRow && (s5PoolLabel || w7PoolLabel || creditsLabel)) {
        quotaMetaRow.hidden = false;
        if (quotaPoolReset) {
          quotaPoolReset.textContent = [s5PoolLabel, w7PoolLabel].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00B7 ');
        }
        if (quotaAiCredits) {
          if (creditsLabel) {
            quotaAiCredits.hidden = false;
            quotaAiCredits.textContent = creditsLabel;
          } else {
            quotaAiCredits.hidden = true;
            quotaAiCredits.textContent = '';
          }
        }
      } else if (quotaMetaRow) {
        quotaMetaRow.hidden = true;
      }

      // AI Credit Overage Guardrail & Vision Bug Alert when 7d reaches 100%
      if (quotaOverageAlert) {
        const overagesOn = Boolean(
          q.weekly7d?.aiCreditOveragesEnabled ??
          q.aiCredits?.overagesEnabled ??
          raw.aiCreditOveragesEnabled ??
          false
        );
        if (q.overageAlert && q.overageAlert.state && q.overageAlert.state !== 'NONE') {
          quotaOverageAlert.hidden = false;
          quotaOverageAlert.className = `overage-guardrail-banner ${q.overageAlert.severity || 'warn'}`;
          quotaOverageAlert.dataset.alertState = q.overageAlert.state;
          quotaOverageAlert.textContent = q.overageAlert.message;
        } else if (w7 >= 100) {
          quotaOverageAlert.hidden = false;
          if (!overagesOn) {
            quotaOverageAlert.className = 'overage-guardrail-banner danger';
            quotaOverageAlert.dataset.alertState = 'LOCKOUT_ALERT';
            quotaOverageAlert.textContent = '7d Quota Exhausted. Enable AI Credit Overages in Settings > Models to prevent session lockout.';
          } else {
            quotaOverageAlert.className = 'overage-guardrail-banner warn';
            quotaOverageAlert.dataset.alertState = 'OVERAGES_ACTIVE_VISION_WARN';
            quotaOverageAlert.textContent = 'Overages Active. Do NOT attach images or screenshots (known upstream HTTP 400 Bad Request bug on overage calls).';
          }
        } else {
          quotaOverageAlert.hidden = true;
          quotaOverageAlert.dataset.alertState = 'NONE';
          quotaOverageAlert.textContent = '';
        }
      }
    } else if (quotaStrip) {
      quotaStrip.hidden = true;
    }

    // Tenth-of-a-Kilotoken parity: headline, Active Signal and Reclaimable cards share one rounding basis.
    const displayParity = computeDisplayTenthsParity(totalTokens, usefulTokens, Math.round(reclaimSumK * 10));
    document.getElementById('usedBig').textContent = fmtTenths(displayParity.displayTotalTenths);
    document.getElementById('usedPct').textContent = `${usedPct}%`;
    const safeLimit = (hasEffortMeta && effortProfile.safeLimit > 0 && (!win.safeLimit || win.safeLimit === 140000))
      ? effortProfile.safeLimit
      : (win.safeLimit > 0 ? win.safeLimit : (effortProfile.safeLimit || 140000));
    const reminderAt = (win.reminderAt > 0 && win.safeLimit === safeLimit)
      ? win.reminderAt
      : Math.round(safeLimit * 0.85);
    const limitK = fmtK(safeLimit);
    const showProfileTag = hasEffortMeta || safeLimit === effortProfile.safeLimit;
    renderLimitLine(safeLimit, win.maxTokens || 200000, showProfileTag ? effortProfile : null);
    const toLimit = safeLimit - totalTokens;
    document.getElementById('freeLabel').textContent = toLimit > 0
      ? `${fmtK(toLimit)} below ${limitK} safe limit`
      : `${fmtK(Math.abs(toLimit))} past ${limitK} safe limit!`;
    renderDelta(raw.delta);

    const usefulWidthPct = Math.min(100, (usefulTokens / maxTokens) * 100);
    const clutterWidthPct = Math.min(100 - usefulWidthPct, (clutterTokens / maxTokens) * 100);

    const barUsefulEl = document.getElementById('barUseful');
    const barClutterEl = document.getElementById('barClutter');
    const barErrorEl = document.getElementById('barError');
    if (barUsefulEl) barUsefulEl.style.width = `${usefulWidthPct}%`;
    // Single source of truth for the history segment state: drives both the bar and the middle card.
    const cacheTtlSec = raw.quota?.cache?.ttlRemainingSec ?? (raw.quota?.cache?.isCacheCold ? 0 : 300);
    const isColdBloat = Boolean(win.historyBarState?.state === 'COLD_BLOAT') ||
      !isCacheTrulyWarmInline(raw.quota?.cache) ||
      (Number(cacheTtlSec) <= 0) ||
      (totalTokens > 179000);
    if (barClutterEl) {
      barClutterEl.style.width = `${clutterWidthPct}%`;
      if (isColdBloat) {
        barClutterEl.className = 'seg-clutter cold-bloat';
        barClutterEl.dataset.barState = 'cold-bloat';
        barClutterEl.dataset.barLabel = 'Reclaimable Cold Bloat';
        barClutterEl.title = 'Reclaimable Cold Bloat';
      } else {
        barClutterEl.className = 'seg-clutter cached-warm';
        barClutterEl.dataset.barState = 'cached-warm';
        barClutterEl.dataset.barLabel = 'Cached History \u2014 95% Discounted';
        barClutterEl.title = 'Cached History \u2014 95% Discounted';
      }
    }
    if (barErrorEl) barErrorEl.style.width = '0%';

    // 3. 3 Beginner-Friendly Breakdown Boxes
    document.getElementById('valUseful').textContent = `${fmtTenths(displayParity.displayActiveTenths)} (${pureRound}%)`;
    document.getElementById('valUseful').style.setProperty('color', '#10b981', 'important');
    document.getElementById('valClutter').textContent = `${fmtTenths(displayParity.displayReclaimableTenths)} (${clutterPct}%)`;
    // Middle card mirrors the history bar segment directly above it: Teal "Cached History" while truly warm,
    // Amber "Reclaimable" when cold or over the 70% horizon.
    const swatchClutterEl = document.getElementById('swatchClutter');
    const lblClutterEl = document.getElementById('lblClutter');
    const clutterCardColor = isColdBloat ? 'var(--hz-clutter)' : 'var(--hz-cached-history)';
    document.getElementById('valClutter').style.setProperty('color', clutterCardColor, 'important');
    document.getElementById('valClutter').dataset.historyState = isColdBloat ? 'cold-bloat' : 'cached-warm';
    if (swatchClutterEl) swatchClutterEl.style.background = clutterCardColor;
    if (lblClutterEl) lblClutterEl.textContent = isColdBloat ? 'Reclaimable' : 'Cached History';
    document.getElementById('subClutter').textContent = !isColdBloat
      ? `95% discounted \u00B7 /compact ready`
      : (clutterTokens > 0 ? `Can be cleared` : `Zero outdated noise`);

    const valFreeEl = document.getElementById('valFree');
    const lblFreeEl = document.getElementById('lblFree');
    const subFreeEl = document.getElementById('subFree');
    const swatchFreeEl = document.getElementById('swatchFree');
    const fmtHeadroom = hasEffortMeta ? (n => ((Math.max(0, n) / 1000).toFixed(1) + 'k')) : fmtK;
    const reasonTip = effortProfile?.limitReason
      ? ` <span class="limit-reason-tip" title="${escAttr(effortProfile.limitReason)}" data-limit-reason="${escAttr(effortProfile.limitReason)}">&#x24D8;</span>`
      : '';
    subFreeEl.title = effortProfile?.limitReason || '';
    if (totalTokens <= safeLimit) {
      lblFreeEl.textContent = 'Safe Headroom';
      if (swatchFreeEl) {
        swatchFreeEl.style.background = 'var(--hz-free)';
        swatchFreeEl.style.borderColor = 'var(--hz-border)';
      }
      valFreeEl.textContent = `${fmtHeadroom(safeLimit - totalTokens)} left`;
      // Neutral white/slate (#f1f5f9) when > 15k left, amber when approaching safe limit, red when over limit
      valFreeEl.style.setProperty('color', (safeLimit - totalTokens) > 15000 ? '#f1f5f9' : '#f59e0b', 'important');
      subFreeEl.innerHTML = `Until ${limitK} safe limit${reasonTip}`;
    } else {
      lblFreeEl.textContent = 'Over Limit';
      if (swatchFreeEl) {
        swatchFreeEl.style.background = 'var(--hz-danger)';
        swatchFreeEl.style.borderColor = 'var(--hz-danger)';
      }
      valFreeEl.textContent = `+${fmtHeadroom(totalTokens - safeLimit)} over`;
      valFreeEl.style.setProperty('color', '#ef4444', 'important');
      subFreeEl.innerHTML = `Past ${limitK} limit${reasonTip}`;
    }

    // 4. Composite Session Risk (Layer 1) & Action Center
    const sessionRisk = evaluateCompositeSessionRiskInline(raw, totalTokens, safeLimit);
    const isOverLimit = totalTokens >= safeLimit || Boolean(amputation.amputationCrisis || telemetry.amputationRisk);
    const isNeedsCleanup = totalTokens >= reminderAt || clutterTokens >= 30000 || pureRound < 65;

    const dot = document.getElementById('statusDot');
    const badge = document.getElementById('statusBadge');
    const prefixLockBadgeEl = document.getElementById('prefixLockBadge');
    if (dot) {
      dot.className = sessionRisk.dotClass;
      dot.dataset.riskBand = sessionRisk.band;
      dot.title = sessionRisk.summary;
    }
    if (selEl) {
      selEl.dataset.prefixLock = String(Boolean(sessionRisk.prefixLockWarning));
      selEl.title = sessionRisk.prefixLockWarning
        ? sessionRisk.prefixLockTooltip
        : "Select which conversation's context memory to inspect";
    }
    if (prefixLockBadgeEl) {
      prefixLockBadgeEl.hidden = !sessionRisk.prefixLockWarning;
      prefixLockBadgeEl.title = sessionRisk.prefixLockTooltip || PREFIX_LOCK_WARNING_TEXT;
    }

    const actionCard = document.getElementById('actionCard');
    const actionIcon = document.getElementById('actionIcon');
    const actionText = document.getElementById('actionText');
    const guidedBox = document.getElementById('guidedStepBox');
    const compactReclaim = document.getElementById('compactReclaim');
    const highEffortBanner = document.getElementById('highEffortSludgeBanner');
    const confBadge = document.getElementById('confidenceBadge');
    if (confBadge) {
      const conf = telemetry.confidence || raw.confidence || 'HIGH';
      if (conf === 'LOW' || conf === 'DEGRADED') {
        confBadge.hidden = false;
        confBadge.style.display = 'inline-block';
        confBadge.textContent = conf === 'LOW' ? 'Low confidence' : 'Degraded';
        confBadge.className = conf === 'LOW' ? 'badge danger' : 'badge warn';
      } else {
        confBadge.hidden = true;
        confBadge.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }

    if (highEffortBanner) {
      if (effortProfile.tier === 'HIGH' && clutterTokens >= 30000) {
        highEffortBanner.hidden = false;
        highEffortBanner.textContent = `\u26A1 High Effort active with ${reclaimSumStr} of stale noise \u2014 compact now to prevent slow reasoning turns and thinking-token burn.`;
      } else {
        highEffortBanner.hidden = true;
        highEffortBanner.textContent = '';
      }
    }

    // Update Top Header Badge and /compact instruction subtitle in all states
    const reclaimLine = (clutterTokens > 0 || reclaimSumK > 0)
      ? `reclaims ~${reclaimSumStr} \u00B7 keeps rules, plan & code intact`
      : '';
    compactReclaim.textContent = reclaimLine;
    if (clutterTokens > 0 || reclaimSumK > 0) {
      badge.textContent = `${reclaimSumStr} can be cleared`;
    } else {
      badge.textContent = 'Healthy';
    }

    // Phase 4 (Layer 4): Dynamic Parameterized /compact, Cache-Aware Advisory & Pre-Compaction Git Drift Warning
    const paramTextEl = document.getElementById('parameterizedCompactText');
    if (paramTextEl) {
      if (raw.compaction?.parameterizedCommand) {
        paramTextEl.textContent = raw.compaction.parameterizedCommand;
      } else {
        const activeFileShortList = dupFiles.length > 0
          ? dupFiles.slice(0, 4).map(f => formatWorkspaceRelativePath(f.path)).filter(Boolean).join(', ')
          : 'active workspace files';
        // Never emit a count placeholder for clause 3; interpolate real sanitized text or omit the clause.
        const corrTexts = (Array.isArray(signals.userCorrectionTexts) ? signals.userCorrectionTexts : [])
          .map(t => String(t || '')
            .split(/\r?\n/).map(l => l.trim()).filter(l => l && !/^\/[a-z][\w-]*\b/i.test(l) && !/^#{1,6}\s/.test(l)).join(' ')
            .replace(/\b[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\/\S+/gi, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim())
          .filter(t => /[a-z]{2,}/i.test(t))
          .map(t => t.length > 90 ? t.slice(0, 89).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '').replace(/[\s,;:.\-]+$/, '') + '\u2026' : t)
          .slice(0, 3);
        const baseCmd = `/compact Focus on preserving: 1) Exported symbol signatures and state scales for [${activeFileShortList || 'active workspace files'}], 2) Current checklist state in task.md`;
        paramTextEl.textContent = corrTexts.length > 0
          ? `${baseCmd}, 3) Explicit user constraints: [${corrTexts.join('; ')}].`
          : `${baseCmd}.`;
      }
    }

    const cacheAdvisoryEl = document.getElementById('cacheCompactionAdvisory');
    if (cacheAdvisoryEl) {
      const ttlVal = raw.quota?.cache?.ttlRemainingSec ?? (raw.quota?.cache?.isCacheCold ? 0 : 300);
      const isColdOrOver179k = Boolean(raw.compaction?.cacheAdvisory?.state === 'COMPACT_NOW') ||
        !isCacheTrulyWarmInline(raw.quota?.cache) ||
        (Number(ttlVal) <= 0) ||
        (totalTokens > 179000);
      if (isColdOrOver179k) {
        cacheAdvisoryEl.className = 'cache-compaction-advisory cold-compact-now';
        cacheAdvisoryEl.dataset.advisoryState = 'cold-compact-now';
        cacheAdvisoryEl.textContent = 'Cache expired. Run parameterized compaction now before firing next turn.';
      } else {
        cacheAdvisoryEl.className = 'cache-compaction-advisory warm-defer';
        cacheAdvisoryEl.dataset.advisoryState = 'warm-defer';
        cacheAdvisoryEl.textContent = 'Cache is currently warm (95% token cost discount). Defer compaction until work pause to avoid 70s execution stall and complete KV cache invalidation.';
      }
      // Mute the /compact instruction's amber accent while the green "Defer compaction" banner is shown.
      if (guidedBox) guidedBox.dataset.cacheAdvisory = cacheAdvisoryEl.dataset.advisoryState;
    }

    const driftBannerEl = document.getElementById('artifactDriftBanner');
    if (driftBannerEl) {
      const handoffAudit = signals.handoff || {};
      const hasDriftWarn = Boolean(
        raw.compaction?.preCompactionWarning ||
        handoffAudit.preCompactionWarning ||
        ((handoffAudit.gitDrift?.dirtyFileCount > 0) && ((signals.activeSignal?.taskMdTurnsBehind || 0) >= 1 || handoffAudit.artifacts?.taskMd?.exists === false))
      );
      if (hasDriftWarn) {
        driftBannerEl.hidden = false;
        driftBannerEl.textContent = raw.compaction?.preCompactionWarningMessage ||
          handoffAudit.preCompactionWarningMessage ||
          `\u26A0\uFE0F Pre-Compaction Drift Warning: ${handoffAudit.gitDrift?.dirtyFileCount || 1} uncommitted workspace file(s) detected via git status --porcelain while task.md is stale. Sync task.md before compacting.`;
      } else {
        driftBannerEl.hidden = true;
        driftBannerEl.textContent = '';
      }
    }

    if (isOverLimit) {
      badge.className = 'badge danger';
      actionCard.className = 'action-card danger';
      actionIcon.textContent = '🚨';
      if (document.querySelector('.action-msg')) document.querySelector('.action-msg').style.display = 'none';
      guidedBox.style.display = 'flex';
      compactReclaim.textContent = reclaimLine;
    } else if (isNeedsCleanup) {
      badge.className = 'badge warn';
      actionCard.className = 'action-card warn';
      actionIcon.textContent = '⚠️';
      if (document.querySelector('.action-msg')) document.querySelector('.action-msg').style.display = 'none';
      guidedBox.style.display = 'flex';
      compactReclaim.textContent = reclaimLine;
    } else {
      badge.className = 'badge';
      actionCard.className = 'action-card';
      actionIcon.textContent = '✓';
      if (document.querySelector('.action-msg')) document.querySelector('.action-msg').style.display = 'flex';
      actionText.innerHTML = `<strong>Memory is clean and focused.</strong> ${pureRound}% of context (${fmtK(usefulTokens)}) is active code and rules with ${fmtK( Math.max(0, toLimit) )} of room before earliest instructions could be affected.`;
      guidedBox.style.display = 'none';
    }

    // 5. Build Actionable Brain Diagnostics Playbook
    const playbookList = document.getElementById('playbookList');
    const reclaimCards = [], spikeCards = [], tipCards = [];
    let cards = reclaimCards;

    // Card A: Duplicate File Reads
    if (hasDupRow) {
      const fileCountStr = dupFiles.length === 1 ? '1 file' : `${dupFiles.length} files`;
      const NOISY = /(^|[\\/])(package-lock\.json|yarn\.lock|pnpm-lock\.yaml|poetry\.lock|Cargo\.lock|dist|build|out|coverage|node_modules)([\\/]|$)|\.(log|min\.js|map|lock)$/i;
      const fileRows = dupFiles.map(f => {
        const noisy = NOISY.test(String(f.path));
        const canIgnore = isIgnorablePath(f.path);
        const ch = chronicOf(f);
        const chronic = isChronic(f);
        const badge = ch && ch.sessions >= 2
          ? `<span class="chronic-badge${chronic ? ' hot' : ''}" title="Seen as a repeated read across recent sessions">&#x21BB; re-read in ${ch.sessions} of last ${ch.windowSessions} sessions</span>`
          : '';
        const ignoreBtn = canIgnore
          ? `<button class="chip-btn${noisy ? ' noisy' : ''}${chronic ? ' chronic' : ''}" data-act="ignore" data-path="${escAttr(f.path)}" title="Add to .antigravityignore">Ignore file</button>`
          : '';
        return `
        <div class="file-subrow rich mono">
          <span class="fpath" title="${escAttr(f.path)}">${formatShortPath(f.path)}</span>
          <span class="fmeta">${f.occurrences} ${f.occurrences === 1 ? 're-read' : 're-reads'} &middot; ${fmtK(f.wastedTokens)} tok</span>
          ${badge}
          ${ignoreBtn}
        </div>
      `;}).join('');

      cards.push(`
        <details class="diag-item diag-rollup" data-slot="collapsible">
          <summary class="diag-top" data-slot="collapsible-trigger">
            <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x1F501; Repeated File Reads (${fileCountStr})</span>
            <span class="diag-impact mono">${fmtKFixed1(dupRowK)}</span>
          </summary>
          <div class="diag-body" data-slot="collapsible-content">
            <div class="diag-desc">Older snapshots of re-read files stay in context; <code>/compact</code> drops them.</div>
            <div class="file-sublist dup-file-sublist">${fileRows}</div>
            <div class="diag-footer-bar diag-actions-row">
              <span class="diag-hint">Prevent re-reads:</span>
              <div class="diag-actions-buttons">
                <button class="mini-btn prompt-btn" onclick="runPrompt('lines', this)" title="${promptTitle('lines')}">${CAN_INJECT ? '&#x26A1;' : '&#x1F4CB;'} Tell agent: Read line ranges</button>
                <button class="mini-btn prompt-btn" onclick="runPrompt('subagent', this)" title="${promptTitle('subagent')}">${CAN_INJECT ? '&#x26A1;' : '&#x1F4CB;'} Tell agent: Use subagent</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <!-- Hidden details drawer element retained for backward-compatible JSDOM assertions -->
            <details class="file-drawer diag-drawer-full" style="display:none;"><summary>Inspect ${fileCountStr}</summary></details>
          </div>
        </details>
      `);
    }

    // Card B: Earlier Chat History (>25 steps ago)
    if (hasOldTurnRow) {
      cards.push(`
        <details class="diag-item diag-rollup">
          <summary class="diag-top">
            <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x1F4AC; Earlier Chat History (${oldTurnCount} old turn${oldTurnCount === 1 ? '' : 's'})</span>
            <span class="diag-impact mono">${fmtKFixed1(oldTurnRowK)}</span>
          </summary>
          <div class="diag-body">
            <div class="diag-desc">Completed steps (&gt;25 turns old) are re-sent every turn; <code>/compact</code> summarizes them while keeping semantically pinned schemas intact.</div>
          </div>
        </details>
      `);
    }

    cards = spikeCards;
    // Card C: Blowup Steps (Single massive outputs >= 8k tok)
    const blowups = signals.blowupSteps || [];
    let spikeIncluded = false, spikePill = 'Retained';
    if (blowups.length > 0) {
      const topBlowup = blowups[0];
      const bp = topBlowup.payload || {};
      const bpKind = bp.kind || 'other';
      const spikeTurns = win.turnCount || sessionMeta.turnCount || 0;
      const spikeStepsAgo = Math.max(0, spikeTurns - 1 - topBlowup.stepIndex);
      spikeIncluded = spikeStepsAgo > 25 || /STALE|ERROR/.test(topBlowup.category || '');
      spikePill = spikeIncluded ? 'Cleared by /compact' : 'Retained';
      cards.push(`
        <details class="diag-item diag-rollup">
          <summary class="diag-top">
            <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x26A1; Large Output at Turn #${topBlowup.stepIndex}</span>
            <span class="diag-impact mono">${fmtK(topBlowup.tokens)} in 1 step</span>
          </summary>
          <div class="diag-body">
            <div class="diag-desc">Turn #${topBlowup.stepIndex} injected ${fmtK(topBlowup.tokens)} tokens in one go.</div>
            <div class="payload-box mono">
              <span class="payload-kind">${bpKind === 'command' ? 'Command that produced this output:' : (bpKind === 'file' ? 'File read into context:' : 'Source of this output:')}</span>
              ${escAttr(bp.detail || formatShortPath(topBlowup.target))}
              <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:4px;">${bpKind === 'file' && bp.path && isIgnorablePath(bp.path)
                ? `<button class="chip-btn" data-act="ignore" data-path="${escAttr(bp.path)}" title="Add to .antigravityignore">Ignore file</button>`
                : `<button class="mini-btn prompt-btn" onclick="runPrompt('truncate', this)" title="${promptTitle('truncate')}">${promptLabel('truncate')}</button>`}${bpKind === 'command'
                ? `<button type="button" class="mini-btn" id="btnRegisterDampeningHook" data-act="dampen-hook" data-command="${escAttr(bp.detail || '')}" title="Registers a fail-open PreToolUse wrapper in .agents/hooks.json (head -n 100 / log redirect) to prevent 20k+ single-turn CLI bloat">&#x1F6E1;&#xFE0F; Register PreToolUse Dampener (.agents/hooks.json)</button>`
                : ''}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="diag-actions" style="margin-top:4px;">
              <span class="retained-badge">${spikeIncluded
                ? 'Included in Earlier Chat History total above'
                : 'In active window (not cleared by /compact)'}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </details>
      `);
    }

    cards = reclaimCards;
    // Card D: Dead Errors / Error Loop
    if (hasErrRow) {
      if (isErrLoop) {
        cards.push(`
          <details class="diag-item diag-rollup">
            <summary class="diag-top">
              <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x1F6A8; Repeated Tool / Command Error Loop</span>
              <span class="diag-impact mono" style="color:var(--hz-clutter)">${fmtKFixed1(errRowK)}</span>
            </summary>
            <div class="diag-body">
              <div class="diag-desc">Repeated failures keep broken approaches in context and can cause loops. <code>/compact</code> or AST Fold collapses them into 1-line tombstones.</div>
              <div class="diag-actions">
                <span></span>
                <button class="mini-btn danger prompt-btn" onclick="runPrompt('loop', this)" title="${promptTitle('loop')}">${promptLabel('loop')}</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </details>
        `);
      } else {
        cards.push(`
          <details class="diag-item diag-rollup">
            <summary class="diag-top">
              <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Fixed Error Logs (${signals.deadErrors?.errorCount || 1} old error${(signals.deadErrors?.errorCount || 1) === 1 ? '' : 's'})</span>
              <span class="diag-impact mono" style="color:var(--hz-clutter)">${fmtKFixed1(errRowK)}</span>
            </summary>
            <div class="diag-body">
              <div class="diag-desc">Dead error output stays in context; <code>/compact</code> or AST Fold collapses them into 1-line tombstones.</div>
            </div>
          </details>
        `);
      }
    }

    cards = tipCards;
    // Card D2: Chronic re-reads across sessions -> ignore or summarise once
    const chronicList = (raw.chronicReads || []).filter(c => c.sessions >= 3).slice(0, 5);
    if (chronicList.length) {
      const rows = chronicList.map(c => {
        const canIgnore = isIgnorablePath(c.path);
        const ignoreBtn = canIgnore
          ? `<button class="chip-btn chronic" data-act="ignore" data-path="${escAttr(c.path)}" title="Add to .antigravityignore">Ignore file</button>`
          : '';
        return `
        <div class="file-subrow rich mono">
          <span class="fpath" title="${escAttr(c.path)}">${formatShortPath(c.path)}</span>
          <span class="chronic-badge hot">&#x21BB; ${c.sessions} of last ${c.windowSessions} sessions</span>
          ${ignoreBtn}
        </div>`;
      }).join('');
      cards.push(`
        <details class="diag-item diag-rollup" id="chronicCard">
          <summary class="diag-top">
            <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x21BB; Chronic Re-reads (${chronicList.length} file${chronicList.length === 1 ? '' : 's'})</span>
            <span class="diag-impact mono">Across sessions</span>
          </summary>
          <div class="diag-body">
            <div class="diag-desc">Re-read in 3 or more recent sessions. Ignore them, or keep a short summary in AGENTS.md so they aren't loaded in full each time.</div>
            <div class="file-sublist">${rows}</div>
          </div>
        </details>
      `);
    }
    // Card D3: Optional read-dedup tooling tip (dismissible; shown only when duplicate reads exist)
    if (dupFiles.length > 0 && !sipTipDismissed()) {
      cards.push(`
        <div class="diag-item" id="sipTipCard" style="padding: 7px 12px;">
          <div class="diag-top">
            <span class="diag-name">&#x1F9F0; Tooling Tip: Read De-duplication</span>
            <button class="tip-dismiss" type="button" onclick="dismissSipTip()" title="Hide this tip">&#x2715;</button>
          </div>
          <div class="diag-desc"><a href="https://github.com/Anuj7411/sipcode" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sipcode</a> (MIT, open source) is an MCP toolkit that catches repeated file reads before they enter context. It is built for Claude Code; compatibility with Antigravity is untested.</div>
        </div>
      `);
    }
    // Card E: User Corrections Detected -> Teach Agent Permanently (Attention-Aware Rule Promotion)
    if ((signals.userCorrections || 0) >= 1) {
      const rulePromo = signals.rulePromotion || {};
      const firstCorrection = Array.isArray(signals.userCorrectionTexts) && signals.userCorrectionTexts.length > 0
        ? signals.userCorrectionTexts[0]
        : '';
      const truncWarnHtml = rulePromo.truncationWarning
        ? `<div class="diag-desc" style="color:var(--hz-clutter);margin-top:3px;">&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; ${escAttr(rulePromo.truncationWarning)}</div>`
        : '';
      cards.push(`
        <details class="diag-item diag-rollup">
          <summary class="diag-top">
            <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x1F9E0; Unlogged User Corrections (${signals.userCorrections} rule candidate${signals.userCorrections === 1 ? '' : 's'})</span>
            <span class="diag-impact mono" style="color:var(--hz-anchor)">Can Teach Agent</span>
          </summary>
          <div class="diag-body">
            <div class="diag-desc">Promote repeated corrections into the Primacy (top 30%) or Recency (bottom 30%) attention zone of AGENTS.md / .agents/CONTEXT.md (checked against the 200-line MEMORY.md truncation threshold).</div>
            ${truncWarnHtml}
            <div class="diag-actions" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;">
              <button type="button" class="mini-btn" id="btnPromoteRulePrimacy" data-act="promote-rule" data-zone="primacy" data-rule="${escAttr(firstCorrection)}" title="Insert directive into Primacy zone (top 30% of AGENTS.md / .agents/CONTEXT.md) and check 200-line MEMORY.md truncation limit">&#x1F3AF; Promote to Primacy (Top 30%)</button>
              <button class="mini-btn prompt-btn" onclick="runPrompt('rule', this)" title="${promptTitle('rule')}">${promptLabel('rule')}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </details>
      `);
    }

    // Card F: MCP Prefix Bloat & Unused Eager Servers Audit (Phase 5 Layer 5)
    const mcpAudit = signals.mcpAudit || raw.baseline?.mcpAudit || null;
    const unusedEagerList = Array.isArray(mcpAudit?.unusedEagerServers) ? mcpAudit.unusedEagerServers : [];
    if (unusedEagerList.length > 0) {
      const eagerRows = unusedEagerList.map(srv => `
        <div class="file-subrow rich mono">
          <span class="fpath" title="${escAttr(srv.configSource || '')}">${escAttr(srv.name)} (eager)</span>
          <span class="fmeta">${fmtK(srv.schemaTokens || 0)} schema &middot; 0 calls in session &middot; switch to <code>lazy</code></span>
        </div>
      `).join('');
      const totalEagerTok = unusedEagerList.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.schemaTokens || 0), 0);
      cards.push(`
        <details class="diag-item diag-rollup" id="mcpAuditCard">
          <summary class="diag-top">
            <span class="diag-name"><span class="diag-caret">&#x25B6;</span> &#x1F50C; Unused Eager MCP Schemas (${unusedEagerList.length} server${unusedEagerList.length === 1 ? '' : 's'})</span>
            <span class="diag-impact mono" style="color:var(--hz-clutter)">${fmtK(totalEagerTok)} static</span>
          </summary>
          <div class="diag-body">
            <div class="diag-desc">These MCP servers are configured as <code>eager</code> in <code>mcp_config.json</code> with zero tool calls across the active session. Switch them to <code>lazy</code> loading to reclaim static prefix tokens.</div>
            <div class="file-sublist">${eagerRows}</div>
          </div>
        </details>
      `);
    }

    const totalCards = reclaimCards.length + spikeCards.length + tipCards.length;
    if (totalCards === 0) {
      playbookList.dataset.sig = '';
      playbookList.innerHTML = `
        <div class="diag-item">
          <div class="diag-name" style="color:var(--hz-useful)">&#x2713; Zero Context Bottlenecks Detected</div>
          <div class="diag-desc">No duplicate file reads, oversized step outputs, or error loops in active memory.</div>
        </div>
      `;
    } else {
      const group = (cls, title, pill, items, metaLabel = '') => items.length
        ? `<div class="diag-group ${cls}"><div class="group-head"><span class="group-title">${title}</span><span class="group-pill mono"${cls === 'reclaim' ? ' hidden style="display:none !important;"' : ''}>${pill}</span>${metaLabel ? `<span class="group-meta mono">${metaLabel}</span>` : ''}</div>${items.join('')}</div>`
        : '';
      const reclaimCategoryPill = `${reclaimCards.length} ${reclaimCards.length === 1 ? 'category' : 'categories'}`;
      const html =
        group('reclaim', 'Cleared by /compact', `${reclaimSumStr} total`, reclaimCards, reclaimCategoryPill) +
        group('spike', 'Biggest Single Turn', spikePill, spikeCards) +
        group('tips', 'Improve future sessions', 'Optional', tipCards);
      // Only rewrite when content changed so click confirmations are not wiped by the 2s live refresh
      if (playbookList.dataset.sig !== html) {
        playbookList.innerHTML = html;
        playbookList.dataset.sig = html;
      }
    }

    // 5b. Active Signal drawer: baseline overhead vs live session work
    const pctOf = n => totalTokens > 0 ? Math.round((n / totalTokens) * 100) : 0;
    const liveWorkTok = Math.min(usefulTokens, signals.activeSignal?.liveSessionTokens ?? Math.round(usefulTokens * 0.5));
    const baseTok = Math.max(0, usefulTokens - liveWorkTok);
    document.getElementById('sigBaseVal').textContent = `${fmtK(baseTok)} (${pctOf(baseTok)}%)`;
    document.getElementById('sigLiveVal').textContent = `${fmtK(liveWorkTok)} (${pctOf(liveWorkTok)}%)`;
    renderBaselineRows(raw.baseline, baseTok);

    // 5c. "What survives /compact" preview (numbers match the reclaimable banner)
    const errCount = signals.deadErrors?.errorCount || 0;
    document.getElementById('surviveDropTitle').textContent = `Compressed (~${reclaimSumStr})`;
    const dropItems = [];
    if (dupFiles.length) dropItems.push(`${dupFiles.length} duplicate file snapshot${dupFiles.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
    if (oldTurnCount) dropItems.push(`${oldTurnCount} turns &gt;25 steps old`);
    if (errCount) dropItems.push(`${errCount} resolved stack trace${errCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
    document.getElementById('surviveDropList').innerHTML = dropItems.length ? dropItems.map(t => `<li>${t}</li>`).join('') : '<li>Nothing to compress</li>';

    // 5d. Handoff readiness: is task.md up to date with recent turns?
    const handoffStatus = document.getElementById('handoffStatus');
    const handoffDot = document.getElementById('handoffDot');
    const btnCp = document.getElementById('btnCheckpoint');
    if (btnCp) btnCp.title = promptTitleRaw('checkpoint');
    const hs = signals.activeSignal;
    if (hs && typeof hs.taskMdTurnsBehind === 'number') {
      const behind = hs.taskMdTurnsBehind;
      const synced = hs.taskMdSaved && behind <= 3;
      handoffState = { synced, behind, saved: hs.taskMdSaved };
      const txt = synced ? '&#x25CF; task.md synced'
        : (hs.taskMdSaved ? `&#x25CF; task.md stale &bull; ${behind} turn${behind === 1 ? '' : 's'} behind` : '&#x25CF; task.md stale &bull; not saved yet');
      handoffStatus.hidden = true;
      handoffStatus.className = 'handoff-status ' + (synced ? 'synced' : 'stale');
      handoffStatus.innerHTML = txt;
      handoffDot.className = 'handoff-dot ' + (synced ? 'synced' : 'stale');
      // Single status dot only — no leading bullet separator that would inherit the green color.
      handoffDot.innerHTML = synced
        ? '<span class="task-sync-badge" id="taskSyncBadge">&#x25CF; task.md synced</span>'
        : `<span class="task-sync-badge" id="taskSyncBadge">&#x25CF; task.md stale (${behind} behind)</span>`;
      btnCp.classList.toggle('emphasis', !synced);
      const drawer = document.getElementById('secondaryDrawer');
      // Keep secondaryDrawer collapsed by default as per UX rollup specification
    } else {
      handoffState = null;
      handoffStatus.hidden = true;
      handoffDot.innerHTML = '';
    }

    // 6. Optional Collapsible 40-Block Timeline
    const turns = sessionMeta.turnCount || win.turnCount || 0;
    const activeBlocks = Math.max(1, Math.min(40, Math.ceil(totalTokens / 5000)));
    const transitionsByBlock = new Map();
    for (const tr of transitions) {
      const tStep = tr.stepNumber ?? tr.turnIndex ?? 0;
      const tIdx = tr.turnIndex ?? tr.stepNumber ?? 0;
      let bIdx = typeof tr.blockIndex === 'number' ? tr.blockIndex : -1;
      if (bIdx < 0) {
        for (let ci = 0; ci < cells.length; ci++) {
          const rng = cells[ci]?.turnRange;
          if (rng && rng[0] >= 0 && tIdx >= rng[0] && tIdx <= rng[1]) {
            bIdx = ci;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (bIdx < 0) {
        bIdx = Math.min(39, Math.max(0, Math.floor((tIdx / Math.max(1, turns || 1)) * activeBlocks)));
      }
      const list = transitionsByBlock.get(bIdx) || [];
      list.push({ ...tr, stepNumber: tStep, turnIndex: tIdx, blockIndex: bIdx });
      transitionsByBlock.set(bIdx, list);
    }

    const mini = document.getElementById('miniTimeline');
    const strandedBannerEl = document.getElementById('strandedSignalBanner');
    const turnFilterPanelEl = document.getElementById('cellTurnFilterPanel');
    mini.innerHTML = '';

    const STRANDED_WARNING_TEXT = 'Stranded in Low-Attention Trough. Risk of recall decay during multi-file reasoning. Re-reference in active prompt or anchor to task.md.';
    const populatedCellCount = Math.max(
      1,
      cells.filter(cellItem => (cellItem?.dominantCategory || 'FREE_HEADROOM') !== 'FREE_HEADROOM' || (Number(cellItem?.totalTokens) || 0) > 0).length || activeBlocks || 1
    );
    const globalFractures = Array.isArray(raw.attentionHeatmap?.fractures) ? raw.attentionHeatmap.fractures : [];
    const strandedTargets = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
      const c = cells[i] || { dominantCategory: 'FREE_HEADROOM', totalTokens: 0 };
      const cat = c.dominantCategory || 'FREE_HEADROOM';

      // Session Fracture Overlay (⚡ between cells where Compaction or Prefix Bust occurred)
      const cellFractures = Array.isArray(c.fractures) && c.fractures.length > 0
        ? c.fractures
        : globalFractures.filter(f => Number(f.cellIndex) === i);
      if (cellFractures.length > 0) {
        for (const frac of cellFractures) {
          const fracEl = document.createElement('span');
          fracEl.className = 'fracture-marker';
          fracEl.dataset.fractureCell = String(i);
          fracEl.dataset.fractureType = String(frac.type || 'FRACTURE');
          fracEl.title = frac.label || `${frac.type || 'Fracture'} at Cell ${i + 1}`;
          fracEl.textContent = '\u26A1';
          mini.appendChild(fracEl);
        }
      }

      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'mini-cell';
      el.dataset.cellIndex = String(i);
      if (cat.includes('ANCHOR') || cat.includes('MEMORY')) el.style.background = 'var(--hz-anchor)';
      else if (cat.includes('ACTIVE')) el.style.background = 'var(--hz-useful)';
      else if (cat.includes('ERROR')) el.style.background = 'var(--hz-danger)';
      else if (cat.includes('STALE') || cat.includes('DIRTY')) el.style.background = 'var(--hz-clutter)';

      // Parabolic U-Shaped Attention Curve A(i) = 1.0 - 4.0 * gamma * (i/N - 0.5)^2 (gamma = 0.6)
      const normRatio = populatedCellCount > 1 ? Math.min(1, i / (populatedCellCount - 1)) : 0;
      const centered = normRatio - 0.5;
      const specFormulaVal = c.parabolicAttention?.specFormulaValue ?? Number((1.0 - 4.0 * 0.6 * (centered * centered)).toFixed(4));
      const uShapedWeight = c.parabolicAttention?.uShapedWeight ?? Number(((1.0 - 0.6) + 4.0 * 0.6 * (centered * centered)).toFixed(4));
      const isPopulatedCell = cat !== 'FREE_HEADROOM' || (Number(c.totalTokens) || 0) > 0 || i < activeBlocks;
      const meetsTroughFloor = populatedCellCount >= 7 && totalTokens >= 44800;
      const inRatioTrough = isPopulatedCell && meetsTroughFloor && normRatio >= 0.30 && normRatio <= 0.70;
      const inCanonicalTrough = isPopulatedCell && meetsTroughFloor && i >= 6 && i <= 15 && i < populatedCellCount;
      const inTrough = Boolean(c.inAttentionTrough) || inRatioTrough || inCanonicalTrough;

      el.dataset.attentionFormula = String(specFormulaVal);
      el.dataset.attentionWeight = String(uShapedWeight);

      if (inTrough) {
        el.classList.add('low-attention-trough');
        el.dataset.attentionZone = 'LOW_ATTENTION_TROUGH';
      } else if (isPopulatedCell) {
        el.dataset.attentionZone = normRatio < 0.30 ? 'PRIMACY' : 'RECENCY';
      } else {
        el.dataset.attentionZone = 'HEADROOM';
      }

      const isStranded = Boolean(c.isStrandedSignal) || (
        meetsTroughFloor &&
        inTrough &&
        (cat.includes('ACTIVE') || cat.includes('ANCHOR'))
      );

      const cellShifts = transitionsByBlock.get(i) || c.effortTransitions || [];
      const shiftTooltip = cellShifts.length > 0
        ? cellShifts.map(t => `Turn #${t.stepNumber ?? t.turnIndex}: Switched effort ${t.fromTier} \u2192 ${t.toTier}`).join(' | ')
        : '';

      const titleParts = [];
      if (isStranded) {
        el.classList.add('stranded-signal');
        el.dataset.strandedSignal = 'true';
        const warnMsg = c.strandedWarning || STRANDED_WARNING_TEXT;
        titleParts.push(`\u26A0 ${warnMsg}`);
        strandedTargets.push({
          cellNumber: i + 1,
          category: cat,
          target: c.topTarget ? formatShortPath(c.topTarget) : `Cell ${i + 1}`,
          warning: warnMsg
        });
      }
      if (shiftTooltip) {
        el.classList.add('effort-shift');
        el.dataset.effortShift = shiftTooltip;
        titleParts.push(shiftTooltip);
        const badgeEl = document.createElement('span');
        badgeEl.className = 'effort-shift-badge';
        badgeEl.textContent = '\u21C4';
        el.appendChild(badgeEl);
      }
      if (titleParts.length > 0) {
        el.title = titleParts.join(' | ');
      }

      el.onmouseenter = () => {
        const cellStepK = Math.round((maxTokens / 40) / 100) / 10;
        const startK = fmtKVal(i * cellStepK).replace(/k$/, '');
        const endK = fmtKVal((i + 1) * cellStepK);
        const zoneTag = inTrough ? ' [Low-Attention Trough 30%\u201370%]' : '';
        document.getElementById('miniInspector').textContent =
          `Block ${i + 1}/40 (${startK}k\u2013${endK}) \u2022 ${cat.replace(/_/g, ' ')}${zoneTag}${c.topTarget ? ' \u2022 ' + formatShortPath(c.topTarget) : ''}${isStranded ? ' \u2022 ' + STRANDED_WARNING_TEXT : ''}${shiftTooltip ? ' \u2022 ' + shiftTooltip : ''}`;
      };

      // Interactive Turn Inspection on Cell Click
      el.onclick = () => {
        mini.querySelectorAll('.mini-cell.selected').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('selected'));
        el.classList.add('selected');
        if (!turnFilterPanelEl) return;
        const cellStepK = Math.round((maxTokens / 40) / 100) / 10;
        const startK = fmtKVal(i * cellStepK).replace(/k$/, '');
        const endK = fmtKVal((i + 1) * cellStepK);
        const turnsDetail = Array.isArray(c.turnsDetail) ? c.turnsDetail : [];
        const rangeLabel = Array.isArray(c.turnRange) && c.turnRange.length === 2
          ? `Turns #${c.turnRange[0]}\u2013#${c.turnRange[1]}`
          : (turnsDetail.length > 0 ? `${turnsDetail.length} turn(s)` : 'No active turns');
        const rowsHtml = turnsDetail.length > 0
          ? turnsDetail.map(td => {
              const tools = Array.isArray(td.toolCalls) && td.toolCalls.length > 0
                ? ` \u2022 tools: ${td.toolCalls.map(escAttr).join(', ')}`
                : '';
              const tgt = td.target ? ` \u2022 ${escAttr(formatShortPath(td.target))}` : '';
              return `<div class="cell-turn-row">Turn #${Number(td.stepIndex ?? 0)} \u2022 ${escAttr(String(td.category || cat))} (${fmtK(td.tokens || 0)})${tgt}${tools}</div>`;
            }).join('')
          : `<div class="cell-turn-row">${escAttr(rangeLabel)} \u2022 ${escAttr(cat.replace(/_/g, ' '))}${c.topTarget ? ' \u2022 ' + escAttr(formatShortPath(c.topTarget)) : ''}</div>`;

        turnFilterPanelEl.hidden = false;
        turnFilterPanelEl.innerHTML =
          `<div class="cell-turn-filter-head">` +
            `<span>Cell ${i + 1}/40 (${startK}k\u2013${endK}) \u2022 ${escAttr(rangeLabel)}</span>` +
            `<span>Attention: ${Math.round(uShapedWeight * 100)}%${inTrough ? ' (Trough)' : ''}</span>` +
          `</div>` +
          (isStranded ? `<div style="color:var(--hz-warn);margin-bottom:4px;">\u26A0 ${escAttr(STRANDED_WARNING_TEXT)}</div>` : '') +
          rowsHtml;
      };

      mini.appendChild(el);
    }

    if (strandedBannerEl) {
      if (strandedTargets.length > 0) {
        const sampleTargets = strandedTargets.slice(0, 2).map(s => `Cell ${s.cellNumber} (${s.target})`).join(', ');
        strandedBannerEl.hidden = false;
        strandedBannerEl.textContent = `\u26A0 ${ sampleTargets }: ${STRANDED_WARNING_TEXT}`;
      } else {
        strandedBannerEl.hidden = true;
        strandedBannerEl.textContent = '';
      }
    }

    // 6b. Multi-Agent (invoke_subagent) Context Tree Pills (Rank #4)
    const subagentPillsEl = document.getElementById('subagentPills');
    if (subagentPillsEl) {
      const invs = raw.subagents?.invocations || [];
      if (invs.length > 0) {
        subagentPillsEl.hidden = false;
        subagentPillsEl.innerHTML = invs.map(inv =>
          `<div class="subagent-pill" title="Workspace mode: ${escAttr(inv.workspaceMode)} | Saved ${fmtK(inv.tokensSaved)} from parent window">${escAttr(inv.badgeLabel)}</div>`
        ).join('');
      } else {
        subagentPillsEl.hidden = true;
        subagentPillsEl.innerHTML = '';
      }
    }

    // 6c. Host Runtime Watchdog Footer Pill (Rank #3)
    const hostBar = document.getElementById('hostWatchdogBar');
    const hostTxt = document.getElementById('hostWatchdogText');
    const btnReap = document.getElementById('btnReapOrphans');
    if (hostBar && raw.hostHealth) {
      const hh = raw.hostHealth;
      hostBar.hidden = false;
      hostBar.className = 'host-watchdog-bar mono' + (hh.status !== 'HEALTHY' ? ' alert' : '');
      hostTxt.textContent = `Host RAM: ${hh.rssGb ?? 0} GB · Orphans: ${hh.orphanCount ?? 0}`;
      if (btnReap) btnReap.hidden = !(hh.orphanCount > 0);
    } else if (hostBar) {
      hostBar.hidden = true;
    }

    const tStr = raw.updatedAt ? new Date(raw.updatedAt).toLocaleTimeString() : new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
    const titleStr = raw.sessionTitle || (window.__PRECOMPUTED_SESSIONS__?.[raw.sessionId]?.sessionTitle) || raw.sessionId?.slice(0, 8) || '';
    const routeBadge = formatEffortRouteLabel(raw);
    const routeMeta = (transitions.length > 0 || hasEffortMeta) ? `[${fmtK(totalTokens)} \u2022 ${routeBadge}] • ` : '';
    document.getElementById('playbookSub').textContent = `${turns} turns analyzed`;
    document.getElementById('footerMeta').textContent =
      `${titleStr ? titleStr + ' • ' : ''}${routeMeta}Turn ${turns} • Updated ${tStr}`;
  }

  // Action Handlers

  (function initPromptButtons() {
    const b = document.getElementById('btnCheckpoint');
    b.classList.add('prompt-btn');
    b.innerHTML = promptLabel('checkpoint');
    b.title = promptTitleRaw('checkpoint');
    b.onclick = (e) => runPrompt('checkpoint', e.currentTarget);
  })();

  // Active Signal drawer toggle
  document.getElementById('btnSignalToggle').onclick = () => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnSignalToggle');
    const drawer = document.getElementById('signalDrawer');
    const open = drawer.hidden;
    drawer.hidden = !open;
    btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
  };

  // Simple / Advanced View Mode Tabs (shadcn/ui Tabs pattern)
  const savedViewMode = (function() {
    try { return localStorage.getItem('ctxtop.viewMode') || 'simple'; } catch { return 'simple'; }
  })();
  function applyViewMode(mode) {
    document.body.setAttribute('data-view-mode', mode);
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-view-tab]').forEach(b => {
      b.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(b.dataset.viewTab === mode));
    });
    try { localStorage.setItem('ctxtop.viewMode', mode); } catch {}
    // Simple mode collapses the parameterized /compact template into a disclosure; Advanced shows it expanded.
    const paramDetails = document.getElementById('parameterizedCompactDetails');
    if (paramDetails) paramDetails.open = mode === 'advanced';
  }
  applyViewMode(savedViewMode);

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-view-tab]').forEach(tabBtn => {
    tabBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const mode = tabBtn.dataset.viewTab || 'simple';
      applyViewMode(mode);
    });
  });

  async function startCleanChat() {
    const title = currentRenderedState?.sessionTitle || 'Session Continuation';
    const sid = currentRenderedState?.sessionId || '';
    const seedPrompt = `Continue work from previous session "${title}" (brain ID: ${sid}). First read the existing artifacts (implementation_plan.md, walkthrough.md, or task.md) in that brain directory to restore context cleanly.`;

    if (window.sidecar?.agent?.startConversation) {
      try {
        showToast(`&#x23F3; Starting a new clean conversation seeded from "${title}"...`);
        await window.sidecar.agent.startConversation(seedPrompt, `Continue: ${title}`);
        showToast(`&#x2713; <strong>Started new clean conversation!</strong> Check your IDE chat list.`);
        return;
      } catch {}
    }
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(seedPrompt);
    showToast(`&#x2713; <strong>Copied handoff prompt!</strong> Open a new chat (or type <code>/clear</code>) and paste (<code>Ctrl+V</code>) to continue with 0 wasted tokens.`);
  }

  // Guardrail: warn before a clean-chat handoff when task.md is stale
  const hideHandoffConfirm = () => { document.getElementById('handoffConfirm').hidden = true; };
  document.getElementById('btnNewChat').onclick = () => {
    if (handoffState && !handoffState.synced) {
      const n = handoffState.behind;
      document.getElementById('handoffConfirmMsg').textContent = handoffState.saved
        ? `task.md hasn't been updated in ${n} turn${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}. Sync before starting a clean chat?`
        : `task.md hasn't been saved in this chat yet. Sync before starting a clean chat?`;
      document.getElementById('handoffConfirm').hidden = false;
      return;
    }
    startCleanChat();
  };
  document.getElementById('btnSyncFirst').onclick = (e) => {
    hideHandoffConfirm();
    runPrompt('checkpoint', document.getElementById('btnCheckpoint'));
  };
  document.getElementById('btnStartAnyway').onclick = () => { hideHandoffConfirm(); startCleanChat(); };

  // Prefix-Safe AST Context Pruner handler (Rank #2)
  const btnGenerateHandoff = document.getElementById('btnGenerateHandoff');
  if (btnGenerateHandoff) {
    btnGenerateHandoff.onclick = async () => {
      try {
        flashButton(btnGenerateHandoff, '&#x23F3; Generating...', 2000);
        const r = await fetch('/api/handoff', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ conversationId: currentRenderedState?.sessionId })
        });
        const data = await r.json();
        if (data && data.success) {
          const rel = data.relativePath || 'docs/HANDOFF.md';
          showToast(`&#x2713; <strong>Generated Baton Pass handoff:</strong> <code>${escAttr(rel)}</code>`);
        } else {
          showToast(`&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; ${escAttr(data?.error || 'Could not generate handoff Markdown.')}`);
        }
      } catch {
        showToast('&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Live daemon not reachable for handoff generation.');
      }
    };
  }

  const btnCondense = document.getElementById('btnCondenseAst');
  if (btnCondense) {
    btnCondense.onclick = async () => {
      try {
        flashButton(btnCondense, '&#x23F3; Condensing...', 2500);
        const r = await fetch('/api/prune', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ conversationId: currentRenderedState?.sessionId, forceHotCache: true })
        });
        const data = await r.json();
        if (data.success) {
          if (data.state) renderState(data.state);
          showToast(`&#x2713; <strong>Condensed ${data.prunedSteps || 0} stale steps (~${fmtK(data.savedTokens || 0)} saved).</strong> Backup saved automatically.`);
        } else {
          showToast(`&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; ${escAttr(data.error || 'Could not prune session.')}`);
        }
      } catch {
        showToast('&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Live daemon not reachable for AST pruning.');
      }
    };
  }

  // Host Zombie Process Reaper handler (Rank #3)
  const btnReapOrphans = document.getElementById('btnReapOrphans');
  if (btnReapOrphans) {
    btnReapOrphans.onclick = async () => {
      try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/host/reap', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({})
        });
        const data = await r.json();
        if (data.success) {
          if (data.state) renderState(data.state);
          showToast(`&#x2713; <strong>Reaped ${data.reapedCount || 0} orphaned process(es).</strong>`);
        }
      } catch {
        showToast('&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Could not reap processes.');
      }
    };
  }

  document.getElementById('btnOpenChat').onclick = async (e) => {
    const targetId = currentRenderedState?.sessionId;
    const selOpt = document.getElementById('sessionSelect')?.selectedOptions?.[0];
    const targetSurface = currentRenderedState?.storeSource || selOpt?.dataset?.surface || 'antigravity';
    const hostSurface = window.sidecar?.surface || latestAutoState?.storeSource || 'antigravity';
    const isForeignSurface = Boolean(targetSurface && hostSurface && targetSurface !== hostSurface);

    if ((e && e.altKey) || isForeignSurface || typeof window.sidecar?.ui?.toggleConversation !== 'function') {
      try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/open-brain', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ conversationId: targetId, surface: targetSurface })
        });
        const data = await r.json();
        if (data && data.success) {
          showToast(`&#x2713; Opened <strong>${escAttr(data.surfaceTag || resolveSurfaceTag(targetSurface))}</strong> folder: <code>${escAttr(formatShortPath(data.brainDir || targetId))}</code>`);
          return;
        }
      } catch {}
      if (isForeignSurface) {
        showToast('&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Cross-surface session: opened folder via daemon fallback.');
        return;
      }
      showToast('&#x26A0;&#xFE0F; Could not open local brain folder.');
      return;
    }
    if (targetId && window.sidecar?.ui?.toggleConversation) {
      window.sidecar.ui.toggleConversation(targetId);
      showToast(`&#x2713; Switched IDE chat view to <strong>${currentRenderedState.sessionTitle || targetId.slice(0, 8)}</strong>.`);
    } else {
      showToast(`Select a conversation from the dropdown above to inspect its memory (or Alt+Click to open its brain folder).`);
    }
  };

  // Handle dropdown conversation selection
  document.getElementById('sessionSelect').onchange = async (e) => {
    selectedSessionMode = e.target.value;
    document.getElementById('feedbackToast').style.display = 'none';
    if (selectedSessionMode === 'auto') {
      if (latestAutoState) renderState(latestAutoState, false);
    } else if (window.__PRECOMPUTED_SESSIONS__ && window.__PRECOMPUTED_SESSIONS__[selectedSessionMode]) {
      renderState(window.__PRECOMPUTED_SESSIONS__[selectedSessionMode], true);
    }
    try {
      const r = await fetch(`/api/state?conversationId=${encodeURIComponent(selectedSessionMode)}`);
      const d = await r.json();
      if (d && d.sessionId === selectedSessionMode) renderState(d, true);
    } catch {}
  };

  // Listen for IDE workspace-change events from /preload.js
  if (window.sidecar && typeof window.sidecar.onWorkspaceChange === 'function') {
    window.sidecar.onWorkspaceChange((uris) => {
      if (Array.isArray(uris) && uris.length > 0) {
        const clean = decodeURIComponent(uris[0]).replace(/\\/g, '/');
        currentWorkspaceBasename = clean.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop() || '';
        if (selectedSessionMode === 'auto' && latestAutoState) {
          renderState(latestAutoState, false);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  try {
    const evtSource = new EventSource('/api/stream');
    evtSource.onmessage = (e) => {
      try { renderState(JSON.parse(e.data)); } catch {}
    };
  } catch {}

  fetch('/api/state').then(r => r.json()).then(renderState).catch(() => {});
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
